     1→"""Fat orchestrator for parallel swarm execution.
     2→
     3→Collapses Rune's microservice architecture into a single-process
     4→orchestrator for local hardware. Coordinates agent supervisors,
     5→training pool, evolution worker, and memory watchdog via asyncio.
     6→
     7→Usage:
     8→    uv run scripts/swarm.py [--config scripts/swarm_config.yaml] [--agents N] [--hours H] [--dry-run]
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import argparse
    14→import asyncio
    15→import json
    16→import logging
    17→import sys
    18→import uuid
    19→from pathlib import Path
    20→from typing import Any
    21→
    22→import yaml
    23→
    24→# Add scripts dir to path for sibling imports
    25→sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))
    26→
    27→from shared.checkpoint_db import SwarmCheckpointDB
    28→from shared.hardware import HardwareProbe
    29→from shared.rune_models import SwarmConfig
    30→from shared.sandbox import get_sandbox_backend
    31→from swarm_evolution import evolution_worker
    32→from swarm_workers import TrainingRequest, memory_watchdog, training_pool_manager
    33→
    34→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    35→
    36→
    37→def load_task_pool(task_source: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    38→    """Load tasks from a JSON file or return empty list.
    39→
    40→    Args:
    41→        task_source: Path to a JSON file containing task definitions.
    42→
    43→    Returns:
    44→        List of task dicts with at minimum 'task_id' and 'description'.
    45→    """
    46→    path = Path(task_source)
    47→    if path.exists():
    48→        with path.open() as f:
    49→            return json.load(f)
    50→    logger.warning("Task source %s not found, using empty task pool", task_source)
    51→    return []
    52→
    53→
    54→async def agent_supervisor(
    55→    agent_id: str,
    56→    tasks: list[dict[str, Any]],
    57→    checkpoint_db: SwarmCheckpointDB,
    58→    training_queue: asyncio.Queue[TrainingRequest],
    59→    dry_run: bool = False,
    60→    max_failures: int = 5,
    61→    shutdown_event: asyncio.Event | None = None,
    62→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    63→    """Tier-2 supervisor for a single swarm agent.
    64→
    65→    Processes tasks from the pool, checking checkpoints for dedup,
    66→    running code via sandbox, and queuing training requests.
    67→
    68→    Args:
    69→        agent_id: Unique identifier for this agent.
    70→        tasks: List of task dicts to process.
    71→        checkpoint_db: Checkpoint DB for dedup and recovery.
    72→        training_queue: Queue for submitting training requests.
    73→        dry_run: If True, skip actual execution.
    74→        max_failures: Consecutive failures before retiring.
    75→        shutdown_event: Event to signal graceful shutdown.
    76→
    77→    Returns:
    78→        Summary dict with completed/failed/skipped counts.
    79→    """
    80→    completed = 0
    81→    failed = 0
    82→    skipped = 0
    83→    consecutive_failures = 0
    84→    sandbox = get_sandbox_backend()
    85→
    86→    for task in tasks:
    87→        if shutdown_event and shutdown_event.is_set():
    88→            break
    89→
    90→        task_hash = task.get("task_id", str(uuid.uuid4()))
    91→
    92→        if checkpoint_db.is_completed(task_hash):
    93→            skipped += 1
    94→            continue
    95→
    96→        checkpoint_db.mark_running(task_hash, agent_id)
    97→
    98→        if dry_run:
    99→            checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task_hash, "dry-run")
   100→            completed += 1
   101→            consecutive_failures = 0
   102→            continue
   103→
   104→        try:
   105→            code = task.get("prompt", "") + task.get("canonical_solution", "")
   106→            test = task.get("test", "")
   107→            script = code + "\n" + test
   108→            result = sandbox.run(script, timeout=30)
   109→
   110→            if result.exit_code == 0 and not result.timed_out:
   111→                checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task_hash, "success")
   112→                completed += 1
   113→                consecutive_failures = 0
   114→                await training_queue.put(
   115→                    TrainingRequest(session_id=task_hash, task_type="swarm")
   116→                )
   117→            else:
   118→                checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task_hash, agent_id)
   119→                failed += 1
   120→                consecutive_failures += 1
   121→        except Exception:
   122→            logger.exception("Agent %s failed on task %s", agent_id, task_hash)
   123→            checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task_hash, agent_id)
   124→            failed += 1
   125→            consecutive_failures += 1
   126→
   127→        if consecutive_failures >= max_failures:
   128→            logger.warning(
   129→                "Agent %s retiring after %d consecutive failures",
   130→                agent_id,
   131→                max_failures,
   132→            )
   133→            break
   134→
   135→        # Backoff on failure
   136→        if consecutive_failures > 0:
   137→            await asyncio.sleep(min(2**consecutive_failures, 30))
   138→
   139→    return {
   140→        "agent_id": agent_id,
   141→        "completed": completed,
   142→        "failed": failed,
   143→        "skipped": skipped,
   144→    }
   145→
   146→
   147→async def run_swarm(config: SwarmConfig, dry_run: bool = False) -> None:
   148→    """Tier-1 orchestrator — runs the full swarm with asyncio.TaskGroup.
   149→
   150→    Args:
   151→        config: Swarm configuration.
   152→        dry_run: If True, skip actual execution and training.
   153→    """
   154→    from adapter_registry import AdapterRegistry
   155→    from sqlmodel import create_engine
   156→
   157→    logger.info("Starting swarm with config: %s", config.model_dump())
   158→
   159→    # Detect hardware
   160→    probe = HardwareProbe.detect()
   161→    budget = probe.compute_budget()
   162→    logger.info("Hardware: %s, Budget: %s", probe, budget)
   163→
   164→    # Initialize registry and checkpoint DB
   165→    registry_engine = create_engine(config.db_url)
   166→    registry = AdapterRegistry(engine=registry_engine)
   167→    checkpoint_engine = create_engine("sqlite:///swarm_checkpoints.db")
   168→    checkpoint_db = SwarmCheckpointDB(checkpoint_engine)
   169→
   170→    # Load tasks
   171→    tasks = load_task_pool(config.task_source)
   172→    if not tasks:
   173→        logger.warning("No tasks to process")
   174→        return
   175→
   176→    # Shared state
   177→    training_queue: asyncio.Queue[TrainingRequest] = asyncio.Queue()
   178→    shutdown_event = asyncio.Event()
   179→    num_agents = min(config.population_size, budget.max_agents)
   180→
   181→    # Partition tasks across agents
   182→    partitions: list[list[dict[str, Any]]] = [[] for _ in range(num_agents)]
   183→    for i, task in enumerate(tasks):
   184→        partitions[i % num_agents].append(task)
   185→
   186→    async with asyncio.TaskGroup() as tg:
   187→        # Agent supervisors
   188→        agent_tasks = []
   189→        for i in range(num_agents):
   190→            t = tg.create_task(
   191→                agent_supervisor(
   192→                    agent_id=f"agent-{i}",
   193→                    tasks=partitions[i],
   194→                    checkpoint_db=checkpoint_db,
   195→                    training_queue=training_queue,
   196→                    dry_run=dry_run,
   197→                    shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
   198→                )
   199→            )
   200→            agent_tasks.append(t)
   201→
   202→        # Training pool (background)
   203→        tg.create_task(
   204→            training_pool_manager(
   205→                training_queue=training_queue,
   206→                budget=budget,
   207→                db_url=config.db_url,
   208→                shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
   209→            )
   210→        )
   211→
   212→        # Evolution worker (background)
   213→        tg.create_task(
   214→            evolution_worker(
   215→                registry=registry,
   216→                interval_seconds=config.evolution_interval,
   217→                shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
   218→            )
   219→        )
   220→
   221→        # Memory watchdog (background)
   222→        tg.create_task(
   223→            memory_watchdog(
   224→                budget=budget,
   225→                agents=agent_tasks,
   226→                shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
   227→            )
   228→        )
   229→
   230→        # Wait for all agents to finish, then shutdown background tasks
   231→        done = await asyncio.gather(*agent_tasks, return_exceptions=True)
   232→        shutdown_event.set()
   233→
   234→        for result in done:
   235→            if isinstance(result, Exception):
   236→                logger.error("Agent failed: %s", result)
   237→            else:
   238→                logger.info("Agent result: %s", result)
   239→
   240→
   241→def main() -> None:
   242→    """CLI entry point for the swarm orchestrator."""
   243→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Rune Swarm Orchestrator")
   244→    parser.add_argument(
   245→        "--config",
   246→        default="scripts/swarm_config.yaml",
   247→        help="Path to swarm config YAML",
   248→    )
   249→    parser.add_argument("--agents", type=int, help="Override population_size")
   250→    parser.add_argument("--hours", type=float, help="Max runtime in hours")
   251→    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Skip execution")
   252→    args = parser.parse_args()
   253→
   254→    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
   255→
   256→    config_path = Path(args.config)
   257→    if config_path.exists():
   258→        raw = yaml.safe_load(config_path.read_text())
   259→        config = SwarmConfig(**raw)
   260→    else:
   261→        config = SwarmConfig()
   262→
   263→    if args.agents:
   264→        config.population_size = args.agents
   265→
   266→    asyncio.run(run_swarm(config, dry_run=args.dry_run))
   267→
   268→
   269→if __name__ == "__main__":
   270→    main()
   271→
```

Excellent! Now let me compile a comprehensive summary based on all the files I've read. Let me create a detailed report:

## Comprehensive Exploration: Rune Agent Loop, Inference, and Evaluation Systems

I've thoroughly explored the Rune codebase. Here's a detailed breakdown of all three systems:

---

### 1. Agent Loop End-to-End (services/rune-agent/)

#### Graph/Node Flow

The agent loop is implemented as a **LangGraph state machine** with 4 nodes:

```
START → generate → execute → reflect → [should_retry] → generate/save_trajectory → END
```

**State Definition** (`state.py`):
- `RuneState` is a TypedDict containing:
  - **Task intake**: `task_description`, `task_type`, `test_suite`, `adapter_ids`
  - **Loop tracking**: `session_id`, `attempt_count`, `max_attempts`
  - **Per-attempt results**: `generated_code`, `stdout`, `stderr`, `exit_code`, `tests_passed`
  - **Accumulated trajectory**: list of step records
  - **Terminal result**: `outcome` ('success' | 'exhausted' | None)

**Node Functions** (`nodes.py`):

1. **generate_node** (async):
   - Reads `RUNE_MODEL` env var (default: "Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B")
   - Takes first adapter from `adapter_ids` if available
   - Builds prompt including task + test suite; on retry includes prior code + error context
   - Calls `inference.get_provider().generate(prompt, model, adapter_id)`
   - Extracts code from markdown block or returns raw text
   - Returns `{"generated_code": extracted}`

2. **execute_node** (async):
   - Concatenates `generated_code + test_suite`
   - Calls `sandbox_backend.run(script, timeout)` (default 30s)
   - Captures stdout, stderr, exit_code, timeout status
   - Sets `tests_passed = (exit_code == 0 and not timed_out)`
   - Returns execution results dict

3. **reflect_node** (async):
   - Increments `attempt_count` (immutably)
   - Appends current step to `trajectory` (immutable list concatenation)
   - Step contains: generated_code, stdout, stderr, exit_code, tests_passed
   - Returns updated attempt_count and trajectory

4. **save_trajectory_node** (async):
   - Determines `outcome = "success"` if `tests_passed` else `"exhausted"`
   - Calls `model_training.record_trajectory()` to persist to disk
   - Returns `{"outcome": outcome}`

**Routing Logic** (`graph.py`):

`should_retry()` conditional edge from reflect:
- If `tests_passed == True` → `save_trajectory` (success termination)
- Else if `attempt_count >= max_attempts` → `save_trajectory` (exhausted termination)
- Else → `generate` (retry)

---

### 2. Inference System (libs/inference/)

#### Architecture

**Provider Interface** (`provider.py`):
- Abstract `InferenceProvider` class defining async API
- Concrete implementations: `VLLMProvider` and `OllamaProvider`
- `GenerationResult` dataclass: text, model, adapter_id, token_count, finish_reason

#### VLLMProvider

**How it works**:
- Uses **OpenAI-compatible API** via AsyncOpenAI SDK for generation
- Uses **vLLM proprietary endpoints** for LoRA management
- Maintains internal set of loaded adapters (works around vLLM bug #11761)

**Methods**:
- `generate(prompt, model, adapter_id, max_tokens)`:
  - If `adapter_id` provided, uses it as model name in API call (vLLM routes to loaded LoRA)
  - Otherwise uses base model
  - POST to `/v1/chat/completions` with OpenAI format
  - Returns GenerationResult

- `load_adapter(adapter_id, adapter_path)`:
  - POST to `{base_url}/load_lora_adapter` with json: `{"lora_name": adapter_id, "lora_path": adapter_path}`
  - Tracks in internal set

- `unload_adapter(adapter_id)`:
  - POST to `{base_url}/unload_lora_adapter`
  - Removes from tracking set

- `list_adapters()`:
  - Returns sorted list from internal tracking set

#### OllamaProvider

**How it works**:
- Uses **OpenAI-compatible API** via AsyncOpenAI SDK
- **No LoRA support** — adapter operations raise `UnsupportedOperationError`

**Methods**:
- `generate()`: Same as vLLM but ignores `adapter_id` (logs warning)
- Adapter ops all raise `UnsupportedOperationError`
- `list_adapters()` returns empty list

#### Factory Pattern (`factory.py`)

**get_provider(provider_type, base_url)**:
- Reads `INFERENCE_PROVIDER` env var (default: "vllm")
- Reads `VLLM_BASE_URL` or `OLLAMA_BASE_URL` env vars (or hardcoded defaults)
- Returns cached instance by (provider_type, base_url) key
- Default URLs: vLLM = `http://localhost:8100/v1`, Ollama = `http://localhost:11434/v1`

**get_provider_for_step(step_config)**:
- Reads provider and base_url from step config dict
- Delegates to get_provider()

#### How to Load and Prompt a Model

```python
# 1. Get provider
provider = get_provider("vllm")  # or "ollama"

# 2. Generate text
result = await provider.generate(
    prompt="Your prompt here",
    model="Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B",
    adapter_id=None,  # or "my-adapter-id"
    max_tokens=1024
)

# 3. Access result
print(result.text)
print(result.token_count)
print(result.adapter_id)
```

For **LoRA adapters** (vLLM only):
```python
# Load adapter
await provider.load_adapter("adapter-001", "/path/to/adapter/weights")

# Use in generation
result = await provider.generate(
    prompt="...",
    model="base-model",
    adapter_id="adapter-001"  # Use loaded adapter
)

# Unload when done
await provider.unload_adapter("adapter-001")
```

---

### 3. Evaluation/Metrics System (libs/evaluation/)

#### Key Functions

**calculate_pass_at_k(n_samples, n_correct, k=1)**:
- Unbiased estimator from HumanEval paper
- Formula: `1 - prod((n-c-i)/(n-i) for i in range(k))`
- Returns probability at least one of k attempts is correct

**run_kill_switch_gate(baseline_pass1, adapter_pass1, threshold=0.05)**:
- Compares baseline vs adapter Pass@1 scores
- Relative improvement >= threshold → "PASS"
- Returns dict with baseline, adapter, relative_delta, verdict

**run_humaneval_subset(adapter_id, subset_size=20, model, completions)**:
- Loads bundled 20-task HumanEval subset from `/data/humaneval_subset.json`
- Expects completions dict: `{task_id: completion_string}`
- For each task: concatenates prompt + completion + test + check(entry_point)
- Executes in subprocess, exit_code 0 = passed
- Returns: pass_count, fail_count, pass_rate, task_results list, summary

**score_adapter_quality(adapter_id, pass_rate, generalization_delta)**:
- If generalization_delta provided: `min(pass_rate + 0.1 * max(gen_delta, 0), 1.0)`
- Else: `pass_rate`
- Returns quality score 0.0–1.0

**compare_adapters(adapter_ids, benchmark="humaneval")**:
- Compares multiple adapters
- Returns: scores dict, rankings list, best_adapter, summary
- Currently returns stub comparison (not fully wired)

**test_generalization(adapter_id, in_distribution_tasks, ood_tasks)**:
- Evaluates on both in-distribution and OOD tasks
- Returns: in_distribution_score, ood_score, generalization_delta, generalizes bool
- Currently returns stub values (not fully wired)

**evaluate_fitness(adapter_id, pass_rate, diversity_score)**:
- Evolutionary fitness: `0.7 * pass_rate + 0.3 * diversity_score`
- Used by evolution operator for selection

#### Out-of-Distribution Benchmark (`ood_benchmark.py`)

**run_ood_benchmark(adapter_id, completions, benchmark_name)**:
- Loads bundled OOD tasks from `/data/ood_tasks.json`
- Executes each completion with test harness
- Returns: adapter_id, benchmark_name, ood_pass_rate, pass_count, total, task_results

**compute_generalization_delta(in_dist_rate, ood_rate)**:
- Returns `ood_rate - in_dist_rate`
- Positive = generalization, negative = overfitting

### 4. Running a Simple Coding Problem Through the System

#### Step-by-step setup:

**Prerequisites**:
```bash
# Install dependencies (using uv as per project rules)
cd /Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune
uv sync

# Set up inference backend (choose one):
# Option A: vLLM (with GPU)
docker run --gpus all -p 8100:8000 vllm/vllm-openai:latest \
  --model Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B

# Option B: Ollama (lightweight)
ollama pull qwen2.5-coder:7b
ollama serve &  # runs on localhost:11434/v1
```

**Minimal Example**:

```python
import asyncio
from rune_agent.graph import get_graph
from rune_agent.state import RuneState
import uuid

async def run_coding_task():
    # 1. Create initial state
    state: RuneState = {
        "task_description": "Write a function that returns the factorial of n",
        "task_type": "function",
        "test_suite": """
def factorial(n):
    if n == 0:
        return 1
    return n * factorial(n - 1)

assert factorial(0) == 1
assert factorial(5) == 120
assert factorial(10) == 3628800
""",
        "adapter_ids": [],  # No adapters for base model
        "session_id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
        "attempt_count": 0,
        "max_attempts": 3,
        "generated_code": "",
        "stdout": "",
        "stderr": "",
        "exit_code": 0,
        "tests_passed": False,
        "trajectory": [],
        "outcome": None,
    }
    
    # 2. Get compiled graph
    graph = get_graph()
    
    # 3. Execute graph
    final_state = await graph.ainvoke(state)
    
    # 4. Check result
    print(f"Outcome: {final_state['outcome']}")
    print(f"Attempts: {final_state['attempt_count']}")
    print(f"Final code:\n{final_state['generated_code']}")
    
    # 5. Access trajectory for training
    trajectory = final_state['trajectory']
    for i, step in enumerate(trajectory):
        print(f"Step {i}: passed={step['tests_passed']}")

# Run it
asyncio.run(run_coding_task())
```

**Environment Variables to Control**:
- `RUNE_MODEL`: Model identifier (default "Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B")
- `RUNE_EXEC_TIMEOUT`: Execution timeout in seconds (default 30)
- `RUNE_EXEC_BACKEND`: "subprocess" or "nsjail" (default "subprocess")
- `RUNE_TRAJECTORY_DIR`: Directory to save trajectories (default ~/.rune/trajectories)
- `INFERENCE_PROVIDER`: "vllm" or "ollama" (default "vllm")
- `VLLM_BASE_URL`: vLLM endpoint (default http://localhost:8100/v1)
- `OLLAMA_BASE_URL`: Ollama endpoint (default http://localhost:11434/v1)

**Using with Ollama**:
```python
# Set env before importing
import os
os.environ["INFERENCE_PROVIDER"] = "ollama"
os.environ["OLLAMA_BASE_URL"] = "http://localhost:11434/v1"

state["task_description"] = "..."  # Ollama model: "qwen2.5-coder:7b"
# Rest is the same — provider abstraction handles it
```

---

### 5. Key Architectural Patterns

#### Workspace Structure
```
rune/
├── libs/
│   ├── shared/           # Sandbox backends, hardware probes, models
│   ├── inference/        # Provider abstraction, vLLM/Ollama
│   ├── evaluation/       # Benchmarks, metrics, Pass@k
│   ├── model-training/   # Trajectory persistence, SFT conversion
│   └── adapter-registry/ # Adapter metadata
├── services/
│   ├── rune-agent/       # LangGraph agent loop (this is the main orchestrator)
│   ├── api-service/      # FastAPI HTTP service
│   ├── evolution-svc/    # Evolutionary adaptation
│   ├── training-svc/     # QLoRA fine-tuning
│   └── lora-server/      # vLLM container with LoRA support
└── infra/
    └── docker-compose.yml # Deployment stack
```

#### Async Pattern
- All nodes are async (async def)
- All inference calls are awaited
- Graph execution via `await graph.ainvoke(state)` or `graph.invoke(state)` for sync

#### State Immutability (LangGraph requirement)
- Nodes return dicts of **updates only**, not full state
- Lists must be concatenated, not appended: `state["trajectory"] + [step]`
- Framework merges updates into state

#### Sandbox Execution
- Code is written to tempfile, executed as subprocess
- Capture stdout/stderr/exit_code
- Timeout support with proper error handling
- nsjail option for stronger isolation (Linux only)

#### Trajectory for Training
- Each step persists: code, stdout, stderr, exit_code, tests_passed
- On success, entire trajectory saved to `~/.rune/trajectories/{session_id}.json`
- `format_for_sft()` converts to chat format for LoRA fine-tuning
- Trajectories enable parametric memory via learned adapters

### Summary: Running a Small HuggingFace Model

To run a simple problem with a small HuggingFace model like `Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B`:

1. **Start inference backend** (vLLM on GPU, or Ollama):
   ```bash
   # vLLM (recommended for LoRA support)
   docker run --gpus all -p 8100:8000 vllm/vllm-openai:latest \
     --model Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B
   ```

2. **Set up environment** (from project root):
   ```bash
   uv sync
   cd services/rune-agent
   ```

3. **Run the agent**:
   ```python
   # Python script or pytest
   import asyncio
   from rune_agent.graph import get_graph
   
   state = {
       "task_description": "Write a sorting function",
       "task_type": "function",
       "test_suite": "assert sort([3,1,2]) == [1,2,3]",
       "adapter_ids": [],
       "session_id": "test-001",
       "attempt_count": 0,
       "max_attempts": 3,
       # ... rest of state fields
   }
   
   graph = get_graph()
   result = await graph.ainvoke(state)
   print(result['outcome'])
   ```

The system will:
- Call generate_node → vLLM generates Python code
- Call execute_node → sandbox runs code + test
- Call reflect_node → records attempt
- Call should_retry → decide retry or save
- Call save_trajectory_node → persists trajectory to disk

All the plumbing is in place for parametric memory via LoRA adapters (on subsequent iterations with trained adapters).

     1→"""Node functions for the Rune coding agent recursive loop."""
     2→
     3→import logging
     4→import os
     5→import re
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from inference import GenerationResult, get_provider
     9→from model_training.trajectory import record_trajectory
    10→from shared.sandbox import get_sandbox_backend
    11→
    12→from .state import RuneState
    13→
    14→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    15→
    16→SYSTEM_PROMPT = "You are a Python code generator. Output only code, no explanation."
    17→DEFAULT_TIMEOUT = 30
    18→DEFAULT_MODEL = "Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B"
    19→
    20→
    21→def _build_prompt(state: RuneState) -> str:
    22→    """Build the user prompt for the LLM based on current attempt.
    23→
    24→    First attempt (attempt_count == 0): includes task description and test suite.
    25→    Retry (attempt_count > 0): also includes prior code, stdout, stderr, exit code.
    26→
    27→    Args:
    28→        state: Current agent state.
    29→
    30→    Returns:
    31→        Formatted prompt string.
    32→    """
    33→    task = state["task_description"]
    34→    test_suite = state["test_suite"]
    35→
    36→    base = (
    37→        f"Task: {task}\n\n"
    38→        f"Test suite (your code must pass these):\n{test_suite}\n\n"
    39→        "Write a Python solution:"
    40→    )
    41→
    42→    if state["attempt_count"] == 0:
    43→        return base
    44→
    45→    prior_code = state["generated_code"]
    46→    stdout = state["stdout"]
    47→    stderr = state["stderr"]
    48→    exit_code = state["exit_code"]
    49→
    50→    return (
    51→        f"{base}\n\n"
    52→        "Your previous attempt produced the following errors:\n"
    53→        f"Code:\n{prior_code}\n\n"
    54→        f"stdout:\n{stdout}\n"
    55→        f"stderr:\n{stderr}\n"
    56→        f"exit_code: {exit_code}\n\n"
    57→        "Please fix the issues and write a corrected solution:"
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→def _extract_code(text: str) -> str:
    62→    """Extract code from a markdown python block, or return stripped text.
    63→
    64→    Args:
    65→        text: Raw LLM response text.
    66→
    67→    Returns:
    68→        Extracted code string.
    69→    """
    70→    match = re.search(r"```python\s*(.*?)```", text, re.DOTALL)
    71→    if match:
    72→        return match.group(1).strip()
    73→    return text.strip()
    74→
    75→
    76→async def generate_node(state: RuneState) -> dict[str, Any]:
    77→    """Generate code for the given task using the inference layer.
    78→
    79→    Calls the LLM (with optional LoRA adapters) to produce a code solution
    80→    for the task description.
    81→
    82→    Args:
    83→        state: Current agent state with task description and context.
    84→
    85→    Returns:
    86→        State update dict with generated_code key.
    87→
    88→    Example:
    89→        >>> state = {"task_description": "Write fibonacci", "task_type": "function",
    90→        ...          "test_suite": "assert fib(5) == 5", "adapter_ids": []}
    91→        >>> result = await generate_node(state)
    92→        >>> 'generated_code' in result
    93→        True
    94→    """
    95→    # Read env vars inside function body so monkeypatch.setenv() works in tests
    96→    model: str = os.environ.get("RUNE_MODEL", DEFAULT_MODEL)
    97→    adapter_id: str | None = state["adapter_ids"][0] if state["adapter_ids"] else None
    98→
    99→    provider = get_provider()
   100→    user_prompt = _build_prompt(state)
   101→    full_prompt = f"{SYSTEM_PROMPT}\n\n{user_prompt}"
   102→
   103→    result: GenerationResult = await provider.generate(
   104→        prompt=full_prompt,
   105→        model=model,
   106→        adapter_id=adapter_id,
   107→    )
   108→
   109→    extracted = _extract_code(result.text)
   110→    logger.info(
   111→        "generate_node: attempt=%d, model=%s, adapter_id=%s, tokens=%d",
   112→        state["attempt_count"],
   113→        result.model,
   114→        result.adapter_id,
   115→        result.token_count,
   116→    )
   117→
   118→    return {"generated_code": extracted}
   119→
   120→
   121→async def execute_node(state: RuneState) -> dict[str, Any]:
   122→    """Execute the generated code in a sandboxed environment.
   123→
   124→    Runs the generated code against the test suite in an isolated subprocess
   125→    and captures stdout, stderr, and exit code.
   126→
   127→    Args:
   128→        state: Current agent state with generated code and test suite.
   129→
   130→    Returns:
   131→        State update dict with stdout, stderr, exit_code, and tests_passed keys.
   132→
   133→    Example:
   134→        >>> state = {"generated_code": "def fib(n): return n", "test_suite": ""}
   135→        >>> result = await execute_node(state)
   136→        >>> 'tests_passed' in result
   137→        True
   138→    """
   139→    # Read env var inside function body so monkeypatch.setenv() works in tests
   140→    timeout: int = int(os.environ.get("RUNE_EXEC_TIMEOUT", DEFAULT_TIMEOUT))
   141→
   142→    script = state["generated_code"] + "\n\n" + state["test_suite"]
   143→    backend = get_sandbox_backend()
   144→    result = backend.run(script, timeout=timeout)
   145→
   146→    stdout = result.stdout
   147→    stderr = result.stderr
   148→    exit_code = result.exit_code
   149→    tests_passed = result.exit_code == 0 and not result.timed_out
   150→
   151→    logger.info(
   152→        "execute_node: exit_code=%d, tests_passed=%s",
   153→        exit_code,
   154→        tests_passed,
   155→    )
   156→
   157→    return {
   158→        "stdout": stdout,
   159→        "stderr": stderr,
   160→        "exit_code": exit_code,
   161→        "tests_passed": tests_passed,
   162→    }
   163→
   164→
   165→async def reflect_node(state: RuneState) -> dict[str, Any]:
   166→    """Reflect on execution results and record the attempt in trajectory.
   167→
   168→    Increments the attempt counter and appends the current attempt's data
   169→    to the trajectory list. Does not make any LLM calls.
   170→
   171→    Args:
   172→        state: Current agent state with execution results.
   173→
   174→    Returns:
   175→        State update dict with incremented attempt_count and extended trajectory.
   176→
   177→    Example:
   178→        >>> state = {"attempt_count": 0, "generated_code": "def fib(n): pass",
   179→        ...          "exit_code": 1, "tests_passed": False, "trajectory": [],
   180→        ...          "stdout": "", "stderr": ""}
   181→        >>> result = await reflect_node(state)
   182→        >>> result['attempt_count']
   183→        1
   184→    """
   185→    step: dict[str, Any] = {
   186→        "generated_code": state["generated_code"],
   187→        "stdout": state["stdout"],
   188→        "stderr": state["stderr"],
   189→        "exit_code": state["exit_code"],
   190→        "tests_passed": state["tests_passed"],
   191→    }
   192→
   193→    # Use list concatenation (not .append()) — LangGraph requires immutable state
   194→    new_trajectory: list[dict[str, Any]] = state["trajectory"] + [step]
   195→    new_attempt_count = state["attempt_count"] + 1
   196→
   197→    logger.info(
   198→        "reflect_node: attempt=%d -> %d, trajectory_length=%d",
   199→        state["attempt_count"],
   200→        new_attempt_count,
   201→        len(new_trajectory),
   202→    )
   203→
   204→    return {
   205→        "attempt_count": new_attempt_count,
   206→        "trajectory": new_trajectory,
   207→    }
   208→
   209→
   210→async def save_trajectory_node(state: RuneState) -> dict[str, Any]:
   211→    """Save the completed trajectory for parametric memory training.
   212→
   213→    Persists the trajectory to disk via record_trajectory() and determines
   214→    the final outcome based on whether tests passed.
   215→
   216→    Args:
   217→        state: Current agent state with complete trajectory and outcome.
   218→
   219→    Returns:
   220→        State update dict with outcome key ('success' or 'exhausted').
   221→
   222→    Example:
   223→        >>> state = {"tests_passed": True, "session_id": "abc", "trajectory": [],
   224→        ...          "task_description": "", "task_type": "", "adapter_ids": []}
   225→        >>> result = await save_trajectory_node(state)
   226→        >>> result['outcome']
   227→        'success'
   228→    """
   229→    outcome = "success" if state["tests_passed"] else "exhausted"
   230→
   231→    record_trajectory(
   232→        session_id=state["session_id"],
   233→        steps=state["trajectory"],
   234→        outcome=outcome,
   235→        task_description=state["task_description"],
   236→        task_type=state["task_type"],
   237→        adapter_ids=state["adapter_ids"],
   238→    )
   239→
   240→    logger.info(
   241→        "save_trajectory_node: session_id=%s, outcome=%s",
   242→        state["session_id"],
   243→        outcome,
   244→    )
   245→
   246→    return {"outcome": outcome}
   247→
```

     1→"""LangGraph workflow for the Rune coding agent recursive loop."""
     2→
     3→from typing import Any, Literal
     4→
     5→from langgraph.graph import END, START, StateGraph
     6→
     7→from .nodes import execute_node, generate_node, reflect_node, save_trajectory_node
     8→from .state import RuneState
     9→
    10→
    11→def should_retry(state: RuneState) -> Literal["generate", "save_trajectory"]:
    12→    """Route from reflect: retry if attempts remain and tests failed, else save.
    13→
    14→    This is a REAL implementation, not a stub:
    15→    - If tests passed -> save_trajectory (success)
    16→    - If attempt_count >= max_attempts -> save_trajectory (exhausted)
    17→    - Otherwise -> generate (retry)
    18→
    19→    Args:
    20→        state: Current agent state after reflection.
    21→
    22→    Returns:
    23→        Next node name: 'generate' to retry or 'save_trajectory' to finish.
    24→
    25→    Example:
    26→        >>> state = {"tests_passed": False, "attempt_count": 0, "max_attempts": 3}
    27→        >>> should_retry(state)
    28→        'generate'
    29→        >>> state2 = {"tests_passed": True, "attempt_count": 1, "max_attempts": 3}
    30→        >>> should_retry(state2)
    31→        'save_trajectory'
    32→    """
    33→    if state["tests_passed"]:
    34→        return "save_trajectory"
    35→    if state["attempt_count"] >= state["max_attempts"]:
    36→        return "save_trajectory"
    37→    return "generate"
    38→
    39→
    40→def create_graph() -> Any:
    41→    """Create and compile the Rune agent workflow graph.
    42→
    43→    The graph follows this flow:
    44→    START -> generate -> execute -> reflect -> [should_retry]
    45→        -> generate (retry) OR save_trajectory -> END
    46→
    47→    Returns:
    48→        Compiled StateGraph ready for execution.
    49→
    50→    Example:
    51→        >>> graph = create_graph()
    52→        >>> graph is not None
    53→        True
    54→    """
    55→    workflow = StateGraph(RuneState)
    56→
    57→    workflow.add_node("generate", generate_node)
    58→    workflow.add_node("execute", execute_node)
    59→    workflow.add_node("reflect", reflect_node)
    60→    workflow.add_node("save_trajectory", save_trajectory_node)
    61→
    62→    workflow.add_edge(START, "generate")
    63→    workflow.add_edge("generate", "execute")
    64→    workflow.add_edge("execute", "reflect")
    65→    workflow.add_conditional_edges("reflect", should_retry)
    66→    workflow.add_edge("save_trajectory", END)
    67→
    68→    return workflow.compile()
    69→
    70→
    71→# Singleton instance for reuse
    72→_graph = None
    73→
    74→
    75→def get_graph() -> Any:
    76→    """Get or create the compiled graph instance.
    77→
    78→    Returns:
    79→        Compiled StateGraph instance.
    80→    """
    81→    global _graph
    82→    if _graph is None:
    83→        _graph = create_graph()
    84→    return _graph
    85→
```

     1→"""Provider factory with instance cache for the inference library.
     2→
     3→Selects between VLLMProvider and OllamaProvider based on configuration,
     4→caching instances by (provider_type, base_url) to avoid redundant construction.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→import os
     8→
     9→from inference.provider import InferenceProvider
    10→
    11→# Module-level constants for default values only; actual env var reads happen
    12→# at call time inside get_provider() so monkeypatch.setenv() works in tests.
    13→_DEFAULT_INFERENCE_PROVIDER = "vllm"
    14→_DEFAULT_VLLM_BASE_URL = "http://localhost:8100/v1"
    15→_DEFAULT_OLLAMA_BASE_URL = "http://localhost:11434/v1"
    16→
    17→_provider_cache: dict[tuple[str, str], InferenceProvider] = {}
    18→
    19→
    20→def _clear_cache() -> None:
    21→    """Clear the provider instance cache.
    22→
    23→    Called by the clear_provider_cache conftest fixture to prevent cache
    24→    state leaking between tests.
    25→    """
    26→    _provider_cache.clear()
    27→
    28→
    29→def get_provider(
    30→    provider_type: str | None = None,
    31→    base_url: str | None = None,
    32→) -> InferenceProvider:
    33→    """Return a cached InferenceProvider for the given backend.
    34→
    35→    Resolves the provider type from the argument or the INFERENCE_PROVIDER
    36→    env var (default: "vllm"). Resolves the base URL from the argument or
    37→    the per-backend env var (VLLM_BASE_URL / OLLAMA_BASE_URL). Instances
    38→    are cached by the (provider_type, base_url) tuple so repeated calls
    39→    with the same arguments return the identical object.
    40→
    41→    Args:
    42→        provider_type: One of "vllm" or "ollama". If None, falls back to
    43→            the INFERENCE_PROVIDER environment variable (default "vllm").
    44→        base_url: Override URL for the backend server. If None, the
    45→            per-backend default env var is used.
    46→
    47→    Returns:
    48→        A cached InferenceProvider instance for the requested backend.
    49→
    50→    Raises:
    51→        ValueError: If provider_type is not "vllm" or "ollama".
    52→
    53→    Example:
    54→        >>> provider = get_provider("vllm")
    55→        >>> isinstance(provider, VLLMProvider)
    56→        True
    57→    """
    58→    ptype = (
    59→        provider_type
    60→        or os.environ.get("INFERENCE_PROVIDER", _DEFAULT_INFERENCE_PROVIDER)
    61→    ).lower()
    62→
    63→    resolved_url: str
    64→    if ptype == "vllm":
    65→        resolved_url = base_url or os.environ.get(
    66→            "VLLM_BASE_URL", _DEFAULT_VLLM_BASE_URL
    67→        )
    68→    elif ptype == "ollama":
    69→        resolved_url = base_url or os.environ.get(
    70→            "OLLAMA_BASE_URL", _DEFAULT_OLLAMA_BASE_URL
    71→        )
    72→    else:
    73→        raise ValueError(
    74→            f"Unknown provider type: '{ptype}'. Supported values: 'vllm', 'ollama'."
    75→        )
    76→
    77→    cache_key = (ptype, resolved_url)
    78→    if cache_key not in _provider_cache:
    79→        if ptype == "vllm":
    80→            from inference.vllm_provider import VLLMProvider
    81→
    82→            _provider_cache[cache_key] = VLLMProvider(base_url=resolved_url)
    83→        else:
    84→            from inference.ollama_provider import OllamaProvider
    85→
    86→            _provider_cache[cache_key] = OllamaProvider(base_url=resolved_url)
    87→
    88→    return _provider_cache[cache_key]
    89→
    90→
    91→def get_provider_for_step(step_config: dict[str, str]) -> InferenceProvider:
    92→    """Return a cached InferenceProvider configured from a step config dict.
    93→
    94→    Reads "provider" and optionally "base_url" from the step config and
    95→    delegates to get_provider(). Designed for use by the agent loop where
    96→    each pipeline step may specify its own provider and server URL.
    97→
    98→    Args:
    99→        step_config: Dict with optional keys:
   100→            - "provider": Provider type ("vllm" or "ollama").
   101→            - "base_url": Override URL for the backend server.
   102→
   103→    Returns:
   104→        A cached InferenceProvider instance for the step's backend.
   105→
   106→    Raises:
   107→        ValueError: If the provider type in step_config is not supported.
   108→
   109→    Example:
   110→        >>> provider = get_provider_for_step({"provider": "ollama"})
   111→        >>> isinstance(provider, OllamaProvider)
   112→        True
   113→    """
   114→    return get_provider(
   115→        provider_type=step_config.get("provider"),
   116→        base_url=step_config.get("base_url"),
   117→    )
   118→
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/scripts/demo_run.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Demo: run a simple coding problem through the Rune agent loop.\n\nUses Ollama with qwen2.5-coder:1.5b to solve a coding task,\nexercising the full generate → execute → reflect → retry loop.\n\nUsage:\n    INFERENCE_PROVIDER=ollama RUNE_MODEL=qwen2.5-coder:1.5b uv run scripts/demo_run.py\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport uuid\n\n# Configure before imports\nos.environ.setdefault(\"INFERENCE_PROVIDER\", \"ollama\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"RUNE_MODEL\", \"qwen2.5-coder:1.5b\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"RUNE_EXEC_TIMEOUT\", \"30\")\n\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s\",\n)\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"demo\")\n\n# Import the agent graph\nfrom rune_agent.graph import create_graph  # noqa: E402\nfrom rune_agent.state import RuneState  # noqa: E402\n\n\nTASK = {\n    \"task_description\": (\n        \"Write a Python function called `two_sum` that takes a list of integers `nums` \"\n        \"and an integer `target`, and returns a list of two indices such that the \"\n        \"numbers at those indices add up to `target`. You may assume each input has \"\n        \"exactly one solution, and you may not use the same element twice.\"\n    ),\n    \"test_suite\": \"\\n\".join([\n        \"# Tests for two_sum\",\n        \"assert two_sum([2, 7, 11, 15], 9) == [0, 1]\",\n        \"assert two_sum([3, 2, 4], 6) == [1, 2]\",\n        \"assert two_sum([3, 3], 6) == [0, 1]\",\n        \"print('All tests passed!')\",\n    ]),\n}\n\n\nasync def main() -> None:\n    model = os.environ[\"RUNE_MODEL\"]\n    provider = os.environ[\"INFERENCE_PROVIDER\"]\n    logger.info(\"Model: %s via %s\", model, provider)\n\n    # Build initial state\n    state: RuneState = {\n        \"task_description\": TASK[\"task_description\"],\n        \"task_type\": \"function\",\n        \"test_suite\": TASK[\"test_suite\"],\n        \"adapter_ids\": [],\n        \"session_id\": str(uuid.uuid4()),\n        \"attempt_count\": 0,\n        \"max_attempts\": 5,\n        \"generated_code\": \"\",\n        \"stdout\": \"\",\n        \"stderr\": \"\",\n        \"exit_code\": 0,\n        \"tests_passed\": False,\n        \"trajectory\": [],\n        \"outcome\": None,\n    }\n\n    logger.info(\"Task: %s\", TASK[\"task_description\"][:80] + \"...\")\n    logger.info(\"Running agent loop (max %d attempts)...\", state[\"max_attempts\"])\n\n    graph = create_graph()\n    final_state = await graph.ainvoke(state)\n\n    # Report results\n    outcome = final_state[\"outcome\"]\n    attempts = final_state[\"attempt_count\"]\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"OUTCOME: {outcome}\")\n    print(f\"ATTEMPTS: {attempts}\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    for i, step in enumerate(final_state[\"trajectory\"]):\n        status = \"PASS\" if step[\"tests_passed\"] else \"FAIL\"\n        print(f\"\\n--- Attempt {i + 1} [{status}] ---\")\n        print(f\"Code:\\n{step['generated_code']}\")\n        if step[\"stderr\"]:\n            print(f\"Stderr:\\n{step['stderr'][:500]}\")\n        if step[\"stdout\"]:\n            print(f\"Stdout:\\n{step['stdout'][:500]}\")\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    if outcome == \"success\":\n        print(\"The model solved the problem!\")\n    else:\n        print(\"The model did not solve the problem within the attempt limit.\")\n\n    return final_state\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    result = asyncio.run(main())\n    sys.exit(0 if result[\"outcome\"] == \"success\" else 1)\n"
}

```
[project]
name = "rune-agent"
version = "0.1.0"
description = "Rune coding agent service — recursive code generation with parametric memory"
readme = "README.md"
requires-python = ">=3.12"
dependencies = [
    "shared",
    "inference",
    "model-training",
    "langgraph>=1.0.6",
]

[build-system]
requires = ["hatchling"]
build-backend = "hatchling.build"

[dependency-groups]
dev = [
    "pytest>=8.0.0",
    "pytest-asyncio>=0.23.0",
    "pytest-cov>=5.0.0",
]

[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel]
packages = ["src/rune_agent"]

[tool.mypy]
strict = true
exclude = ["venv", ".venv", "docs"]
plugins = []

ATTEMPTS: 1
============================================================

--- Attempt 1 [PASS] ---
Code:
def two_sum(nums, target):
    num_dict = {}
    for i, num in enumerate(nums):
        complement = target - num
        if complement in num_dict:
            return [num_dict[complement], i]
        num_dict[num] = i
    return None
Stdout:
All tests passed!


============================================================
The model solved the problem!

> AGENT

**It worked on the first attempt!** Here's what happened:

1. **All 5 branches merged to main** — all CI checks pass (ruff, format, mypy, 211 tests)
2. **Ollama installed** with `qwen2.5-coder:1.5b` (986MB model)
3. **Full Rune agent loop executed end-to-end**:
   - `generate_node` → called Ollama, got a hash-map based `two_sum` solution (266 tokens)
   - `execute_node` → ran code + tests in sandbox → all tests passed
   - `reflect_node` → recorded the attempt in trajectory
   - `save_trajectory_node` → saved trajectory to disk for future LoRA training

The 1.5B model solved the classic two-sum problem in one shot with a clean O(n) hash map solution. The full loop took ~23 seconds (mostly model inference on the first cold call). The trajectory is now saved and could be used for parametric memory training.

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/scripts/demo_project.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Demo: challenge the model with a full web app project.\n\nAsks the model to build a complete single-file FastAPI REST API\n(URL shortener) with multiple endpoints, tested via TestClient.\n\nUsage:\n    uv run scripts/demo_project.py\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport uuid\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Add workspace src dirs\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\nfor src_dir in [\n    \"services/rune-agent/src\",\n    \"libs/inference/src\",\n    \"libs/shared/src\",\n    \"libs/model-training/src\",\n    \"libs/adapter-registry/src\",\n    \"libs/evaluation/src\",\n]:\n    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / src_dir))\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"INFERENCE_PROVIDER\", \"ollama\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"RUNE_MODEL\", \"qwen2.5-coder:1.5b\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"RUNE_EXEC_TIMEOUT\", \"30\")\n\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s\",\n)\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"demo\")\n\nfrom rune_agent.graph import create_graph  # noqa: E402\nfrom rune_agent.state import RuneState  # noqa: E402\n\nTASK = {\n    \"task_description\": \"\"\"Build a complete URL shortener REST API as a single Python file using FastAPI.\n\nRequirements:\n1. A FastAPI app stored in a variable called `app`\n2. An in-memory dict called `url_store` mapping short codes to URLs\n3. A `POST /shorten` endpoint that accepts JSON `{\"url\": \"...\"}` and returns `{\"short_code\": \"...\", \"url\": \"...\"}`. Generate a random 6-character alphanumeric short code.\n4. A `GET /{short_code}` endpoint that returns `{\"short_code\": \"...\", \"url\": \"...\"}` for a valid code, or raises 404 with `{\"detail\": \"Not found\"}` for invalid codes.\n5. A `GET /stats/count` endpoint that returns `{\"count\": N}` with the total number of stored URLs.\n6. A `DELETE /{short_code}` endpoint that deletes a URL and returns `{\"deleted\": true}`, or raises 404.\n\nUse pydantic BaseModel for request/response schemas. Import FastAPI, BaseModel, HTTPException from fastapi and pydantic.\"\"\",\n\n    \"test_suite\": \"\"\"\n# --- Tests using FastAPI TestClient ---\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nclient = TestClient(app)\n\n# Test 1: POST /shorten creates a short URL\nresp = client.post(\"/shorten\", json={\"url\": \"https://example.com\"})\nassert resp.status_code == 200, f\"POST /shorten failed: {resp.status_code} {resp.text}\"\ndata = resp.json()\nassert \"short_code\" in data, f\"Missing short_code in response: {data}\"\nassert data[\"url\"] == \"https://example.com\"\nassert len(data[\"short_code\"]) == 6\ncode1 = data[\"short_code\"]\n\n# Test 2: GET /{short_code} retrieves the URL\nresp = client.get(f\"/{code1}\")\nassert resp.status_code == 200, f\"GET /{code1} failed: {resp.status_code}\"\nassert resp.json()[\"url\"] == \"https://example.com\"\n\n# Test 3: GET with invalid code returns 404\nresp = client.get(\"/ZZZZZZ\")\nassert resp.status_code == 404, f\"Expected 404, got {resp.status_code}\"\n\n# Test 4: POST another URL and check stats\nresp = client.post(\"/shorten\", json={\"url\": \"https://python.org\"})\nassert resp.status_code == 200\ncode2 = resp.json()[\"short_code\"]\n\nresp = client.get(\"/stats/count\")\nassert resp.status_code == 200, f\"GET /stats/count failed: {resp.status_code}\"\nassert resp.json()[\"count\"] == 2, f\"Expected count 2, got {resp.json()}\"\n\n# Test 5: DELETE removes a URL\nresp = client.delete(f\"/{code1}\")\nassert resp.status_code == 200\nassert resp.json()[\"deleted\"] is True\n\n# Verify it's gone\nresp = client.get(f\"/{code1}\")\nassert resp.status_code == 404\n\n# Verify count decreased\nresp = client.get(\"/stats/count\")\nassert resp.json()[\"count\"] == 1\n\n# Test 6: DELETE invalid code returns 404\nresp = client.delete(\"/ZZZZZZ\")\nassert resp.status_code == 404\n\nprint(\"All 6 tests passed! URL shortener API works end-to-end.\")\n\"\"\",\n}\n\n\nasync def main() -> None:\n    model = os.environ[\"RUNE_MODEL\"]\n    logger.info(\"Model: %s — Challenge: URL Shortener REST API\", model)\n\n from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from pydantic import BaseModel
import uuid
import random
import string

app = FastAPI()

url_store = {}

class URLItem(BaseModel):
    url: str

def generate_short_code():
    while True:
        code = ''.join(random.choices(string.ascii_letters + string.digits, k=6))
        if code not in url_store:
            return code

@app.post("/shorten")
async def shorten_url(urlItem: URLItem):
    code = generate_short_code()
    url_store[code] = urlItem.url
    return {"short_code": code, "url": urlItem.url}

@app.get("/{short_code}")
async def get_url(short_code: str):
    if shortcut_exists(short_code):
        return url_store[short_code]
    else:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Not found")

@app.get("/stats/count")
async def get_stats():
    return {"count": len(url_store)}

@app.delete("/{short_code}")
async def delete_url(request: Request, short_code: str):
    if shortcut_exists(short_code):
        del url_store[short_code]
        return {"deleted": True}
    else:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Not found")

def shortcut_exists(short_code):
    return short_code in url_store

Interesting — the 1.5B model got very close! Two recurring issues: (1) missing `import string` on attempt 1, and (2) `GET /{short_code}` returning just the URL string instead of `{"short_code": ..., "url": ...}`. Also, FastAPI's route ordering matters — `/stats/count` gets captured by `/{short_code}` first.

Let me pull the 3B model and try again — it should handle these subtleties better.
