     1→# RFC: Parallel Swarm Execution for Rune v6.0 — Revised
     2→
     3→**Author:** AI Architect
     4→**Date:** 2026-03-12
     5→**Status:** DRAFT v2
     6→**Supersedes:** `swarm-execution-proposal.md` (v1)
     7→**Milestone Target:** v6.0
     8→
     9→---
    10→
    11→## 1. Motivation (unchanged from v1)
    12→
    13→Karpathy's autoresearch demonstrated ~700 autonomous iterations overnight producing ~11% cumulative gain. AgentHub extends this to multi-agent swarms via shared message boards and git DAGs.
    14→
    15→Rune rejects context-window coordination. Agents don't read prior solutions — they **load** them as LoRA adapters. The adapter-registry is the message board. The evolution-svc is the pull request review. Weight-space is the coordination substrate.
    16→
    17→**What changed in v2:** v1 proposed a microservices architecture (5 FastAPI services + Redis + Docker sandboxes). This revision collapses everything into a single-process "fat orchestrator" to minimize overhead on local hardware — the only surviving HTTP boundary is to vLLM, which must own the GPU as a separate process.
    18→
    19→---
    20→
    21→## 2. Architecture Overview
    22→
    23→```
    24→┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    25→│                    swarm.py (single process)                             │
    26→│                                                                          │
    27→│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │
    28→│  │                    Orchestrator (asyncio event loop)                 │ │
    29→│  │                                                                     │ │
    30→│  │  ┌─────────────┐  ┌─────────────┐       ┌─────────────┐            │ │
    31→│  │  │ Agent-0     │  │ Agent-1     │  ...  │ Agent-N     │            │ │
    32→│  │  │ supervisor  │  │ supervisor  │       │ supervisor  │            │ │
    33→│  │  │ ┌─────────┐ │  │ ┌─────────┐ │       │ ┌─────────┐ │            │ │
    34→│  │  │ │LangGraph│ │  │ │LangGraph│ │       │ │LangGraph│ │            │ │
    35→│  │  │ │  loop   │ │  │ │  loop   │ │       │ │  loop   │ │            │ │
    36→│  │  │ └────┬────┘ │  │ └────┬────┘ │       │ └────┬────┘ │            │ │
    37→│  │  │      │nsjail│  │      │nsjail│       │      │nsjail│            │ │
    38→│  │  └──────┼──────┘  └──────┼──────┘       └──────┼──────┘            │ │
    39→│  │         │                │                      │                   │ │
    40→│  │  ┌──────▼────────────────▼──────────────────────▼──────┐            │ │
    41→│  │  │              asyncio.Queue (trajectories)           │            │ │
    42→│  │  └──────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┘            │ │
    43→│  │                         │                                           │ │
    44→│  │  ┌──────────────────────▼──────────────────────────────┐            │ │
    45→│  │  │         Training Workers (ProcessPoolExecutor)       │            │ │
    46→│  │  │         M = num_gpus, GPU-bound, out-of-process      │            │ │
    47→│  │  └──────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┘            │ │
    48→│  │                         │                                           │ │
    49→│  │  ┌──────────────────────▼──────────────────────────────┐            │ │
    50→│  │  │         Evolution Worker (asyncio task, periodic)     │            │ │
    51→│  │  │         evaluate → rank → merge → prune               │            │ │
    52→│  │  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘            │ │
    53→│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
    54→│                                                                          │
    55→│  ┌───────────────────────┐  ┌────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
    56→│  │ AdapterRegistry       │  │  HardwareProbe                         │  │
    57→│  │ (direct SQLite calls) │  │  (startup: CPU, RAM, VRAM → budget)   │  │
    58→│  └───────────────────────┘  └────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
    59→└──────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    60→               │ HTTP (only external boundary)
    61→               ▼
    62→┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    63→│                        lora-server (vLLM, separate process)             │
    64→│  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │
    65→│  │  sleep(level=1) ←→ wake_up()  cycle for GPU time-sharing          │ │
    66→│  │  ┌──────┐ ┌──────┐ ┌──────┐     ┌──────┐                         │ │
    67→│  │  │LoRA-A│ │LoRA-B│ │LoRA-C│ ... │ base │                         │ │
    68→│  │  └──────┘ └──────┘ └──────┘     └──────┘                         │ │
    69→│  └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
    70→└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    71→```
    72→
    73→**What's eliminated vs v1:** 3 FastAPI processes (training-svc, evolution-svc, api-service), Redis, all inter-service HTTP serialization. Estimated savings: ~300MB RAM, ~200ms per training job dispatch.
    74→
    75→---
    76→
    77→## 3. Two-Tier Exception Hierarchy
    78→
    79→This is the critical design piece that makes the swarm resilient without `TaskGroup`'s fail-fast behavior.
    80→
    81→### 3.1 The Problem with `asyncio.TaskGroup`
    82→
    83→`TaskGroup` propagates exceptions: if Agent-3 hits an unhandled error, it cancels all 7 other agents via `ExceptionGroup`. This is correct for transactional work; it's catastrophic for a swarm where agents are independent.
    84→
    85→### 3.2 Solution: Supervisor Loop per Agent + Orchestrator TaskGroup
    86→
    87→```
    88→Tier 1 — Orchestrator Level (TaskGroup, fail-fast):
    89→  ├── dispatcher task        — if this dies, the swarm is broken
    90→  ├── training pool manager  — if this dies, no training can happen
    91→  ├── evolution worker       — if this dies, no pruning/merging
    92→  └── agent supervisors × N  — these NEVER propagate up
    93→
    94→Tier 2 — Agent Supervisor Level (try/except, restart with backoff):
    95→  └── agent_supervisor(agent_id=3)
    96→      ├── try: run LangGraph loop on next task
    97→      ├── except: log failure, mark task as failed in SQLite
    98→      ├── backoff: 2^attempt seconds (capped at 60s)
    99→      └── restart: pick next task from queue, continue
   100→```
   101→
   102→### 3.3 Implementation
   103→
   104→```python
   105→# scripts/swarm.py
   106→
   107→async def agent_supervisor(
   108→    agent_id: int,
   109→    task_queue: asyncio.Queue,
   110→    registry: AdapterRegistry,
   111→    checkpoint_db: SwarmCheckpointDB,
   112→    graph: CompiledStateGraph,
   113→    vllm_client: VLLMProvider,
   114→    training_queue: asyncio.Queue,
   115→    max_consecutive_failures: int = 5,
   116→):
   117→    """Tier-2 supervisor: restarts agent on failure, never propagates."""
   118→    consecutive_failures = 0
   119→    backoff = 1.0
   120→
   121→    while not task_queue.empty():
   122→        task = await task_queue.get()
   123→        checkpoint_db.mark_running(task.hash, agent_id)
   124→
   125→        try:
   126→            # Weight-space discovery: query registry, not a message board
   127→            best = registry.query_best_for_task(task.task_type, top_k=1)
   128→            adapter_ids = [best[0].id] if best else []
   129→
   130→            result = await graph.ainvoke({
   131→                "task_description": task.description,
   132→                "task_type": task.task_type,
   133→                "test_suite": task.test_suite,
   134→                "adapter_ids": adapter_ids,
   135→                "max_attempts": 5,
   136→                "session_id": f"swarm-{agent_id}-{task.hash[:8]}",
   137→            })
   138→
   139→            if result["outcome"] == "success":
   140→                await training_queue.put(TrainingRequest(
   141→                    session_id=result["session_id"],
   142→                    task_type=task.task_type,
   143→                ))
   144→
   145→            checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task.hash, result["outcome"])
   146→            consecutive_failures = 0
   147→            backoff = 1.0
   148→
   149→        except Exception:
   150→            logger.exception("Agent %d failed on task %s", agent_id, task.hash)
   151→            checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task.hash, agent_id)
   152→            consecutive_failures += 1
   153→
   154→            if consecutive_failures >= max_consecutive_failures:
   155→                logger.error(
   156→                    "Agent %d: %d consecutive failures, retiring",
   157→                    agent_id, consecutive_failures,
   158→                )
   159→                return  # graceful exit, orchestrator TaskGroup continues
   160→
   161→            await asyncio.sleep(min(backoff, 60.0))
   162→            backoff *= 2
   163→
   164→
   165→async def run_swarm(config: SwarmConfig):
   166→    """Tier-1 orchestrator: TaskGroup over critical infrastructure."""
   167→    hw = HardwareProbe.detect()
   168→    registry = AdapterRegistry(engine=create_engine(config.db_url))
   169→    checkpoint_db = SwarmCheckpointDB(config.db_url)
   170→    task_queue = asyncio.Queue()
   171→    training_queue = asyncio.Queue()
   172→    graph = get_graph()
   173→
   174→    for task in load_task_pool(config.task_source):
   175→        if not checkpoint_db.is_completed(task.hash):
   176→            await task_queue.put(task)
   177→
   178→    async with asyncio.TaskGroup() as tg:
   179→        # Tier-1: critical infrastructure (fail-fast)
   180→        tg.create_task(training_pool_manager(
   181→            training_queue, registry, hw,
   182→        ))
   183→        tg.create_task(evolution_worker(
   184→            registry, config.evolution_interval,
   185→        ))
   186→
   187→        # Tier-2: agent supervisors (fault-isolated)
   188→        for i in range(hw.budget.max_agents):
   189→            tg.create_task(agent_supervisor(
   190→                agent_id=i,
   191→                task_queue=task_queue,
   192→                registry=registry,
   193→                checkpoint_db=checkpoint_db,
   194→                graph=graph,
   195→                vllm_client=get_provider(),
   196→                training_queue=training_queue,
   197→            ))
   198→```
   199→
   200→Note: agent supervisor tasks are launched inside the TaskGroup but they internally catch all exceptions and never let them propagate. They either restart (backoff) or return gracefully. The TaskGroup only tears down if a Tier-1 component (dispatcher, training pool, evolution worker) fails — which correctly means the swarm infrastructure is broken.
   201→
   202→---
   203→
   204→## 4. Training Workers via ProcessPoolExecutor
   205→
   206→### 4.1 The Problem
   207→
   208→QLoRA fine-tuning is GPU-bound and CPU-blocking. It calls into CUDA kernels via PyTorch, which holds the GIL during GPU synchronization. Running this in an asyncio coroutine would stall the entire event loop — freezing all agents.
   209→
   210→### 4.2 Solution
   211→
   212→```python
   213→# scripts/swarm.py
   214→
   215→async def training_pool_manager(
   216→    training_queue: asyncio.Queue,
   217→    registry: AdapterRegistry,
   218→    hw: HardwareProbe,
   219→):
   220→    """Tier-1: manages out-of-process training workers."""
   221→    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
   222→
   223→    # M = number of training GPUs (typically 1 on local hardware)
   224→    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=hw.budget.training_slots) as pool:
   225→        while True:
   226→            request = await training_queue.get()
   227→
   228→            # Submit to separate process — does not block event loop
   229→            future = loop.run_in_executor(
   230→                pool,
   231→                _train_in_subprocess,  # top-level function (picklable)
   232→                request.session_id,
   233→                request.task_type,
   234→                str(hw.adapter_dir),
   235→                str(hw.db_url),
   236→            )
   237→
   238→            # Fire-and-forget with callback
   239→            future.add_done_callback(
   240→                lambda f: _on_training_complete(f, registry)
   241→            )
   242→
   243→
   244→def _train_in_subprocess(
   245→    session_id: str,
   246→    task_type: str,
   247→    adapter_dir: str,
   248→    db_url: str,
   249→) -> str:
   250→    """Runs in separate process. Must be a top-level function (pickle)."""
   251→    # Deferred GPU imports — only this process loads CUDA
   252→    from model_training.trainer import train_and_register
   253→    return train_and_register(
   254→        session_id=session_id,
   255→        task_type=task_type,
   256→        adapter_dir=adapter_dir,
   257→        db_url=db_url,
   258→    )
   259→```
   260→
   261→### 4.3 Why Not Threads
   262→
   263→`ThreadPoolExecutor` would share the GIL. PyTorch's GPU synchronization calls (`torch.cuda.synchronize()`, `.backward()`, `.step()`) hold the GIL intermittently, causing micro-stalls across all agent coroutines. `ProcessPoolExecutor` fully isolates the CUDA context.
   264→
   265→### 4.4 Constraint: Training and Inference Cannot Share a Single GPU Simultaneously
   266→
   267→On single-GPU hardware, vLLM holds the GPU for inference while training needs it for backward passes. This is where sleep mode comes in (Section 5).
   268→
   269→---
   270→
   271→## 5. GPU Time-Sharing via vLLM sleep()/wake_up()
   272→
   273→### 5.1 The Mechanism
   274→
   275→vLLM's sleep mode (introduced 2025) allows the inference server to temporarily release GPU memory without a cold restart:
   276→
   277→| Level | What's freed | Wake latency | Use case |
   278→|---|---|---|---|
   279→| Level 1 | KV cache; weights offloaded to CPU | ~0.26s | Short training step, weights reused |
   280→| Level 2 | KV cache + weights discarded | ~0.85s | Full QLoRA fine-tune, weights updated |
   281→| Cold restart | Everything | ~37.6s | — |
   282→
   283→Level 1 wake is **145× faster** than cold restart. Level 2 is **44× faster**. This is proven in HuggingFace TRL's GRPO pipeline, where the training loop calls `sleep(level=2)` → train → `wake_up()` every iteration.
   284→
   285→### 5.2 Integration into the Swarm
   286→
   287→On single-GPU hardware, the training pool manager coordinates GPU access:
   288→
   289→```python
   290→async def training_pool_manager(training_queue, registry, hw):
   291→    """Single-GPU path: sleep vLLM → train → wake vLLM."""
   292→    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
   293→    vllm_base = os.environ.get("VLLM_BASE_URL", "http://localhost:8100")
   294→
   295→    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:
   296→        while True:
   297→            # Batch: collect pending training requests (up to batch_size)
   298→            batch = await _drain_queue(training_queue, max_batch=4, timeout=30.0)
   299→            if not batch:
   300→                continue
   301→
   302→            # Phase 1: Put vLLM to sleep (agents block on generate() until wake)
   303→            async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
   304→                await client.post(f"{vllm_base}/sleep?level=1")
   305→
   306→            try:
   307→                # Phase 2: Train in subprocess (GPU is free)
   308→                for request in batch:
   309→                    adapter_id = await loop.run_in_executor(
   310→                        pool,
   311→                        _train_in_subprocess,
   312→                        request.session_id,
   313→                        request.task_type,
   314→                        str(hw.adapter_dir),
   315→                        str(hw.db_url),
   316→                    )
   317→                    registry.update_fitness(adapter_id, ...)
   318→            finally:
   319→                # Phase 3: Wake vLLM (agents resume)
   320→                async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
   321→                    await client.post(f"{vllm_base}/wake_up")
   322→```
   323→
   324→### 5.3 Multi-GPU Path
   325→
   326→On 2+ GPU hardware, dedicate GPU 0 to vLLM (always awake) and GPU 1+ to training (always available). No sleep/wake needed — full parallelism.
   327→
   328→```python
   329→if hw.budget.training_slots > 0 and hw.num_gpus >= 2:
   330→    # Multi-GPU: vLLM on GPU 0, training on GPU 1+
   331→    os.environ["CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES"] = ",".join(
   332→        str(i) for i in range(1, hw.num_gpus)
   333→    )
   334→    # No sleep/wake coordination needed
   335→else:
   336→    # Single-GPU: time-share via sleep/wake
   337→    # (code above)
   338→```
   339→
   340→### 5.4 Agent-Side Impact
   341→
   342→During the sleep window, agents calling `provider.generate()` will block on vLLM's HTTP endpoint (connection held open, server unresponsive). This is desirable — agents naturally pause during training without explicit coordination. When vLLM wakes, queued requests resume.
   343→
   344→Agents performing non-inference work (executing code via nsjail, recording trajectories, querying the registry) are unaffected by the sleep window.
   345→
   346→---
   347→
   348→## 6. Code Execution Sandbox: nsjail
   349→
   350→### 6.1 Why nsjail Over bubblewrap
   351→
   352→Both provide Linux namespace isolation. nsjail adds:
   353→- `--rlimit_as MB` — hard memory cap per execution (prevents OOM from agent-generated code)
   354→- `--rlimit_cpu SEC` — CPU time limit (prevents infinite loops that `timeout` misses for multi-threaded code)
   355→- `--time_limit SEC` — wall-time limit
   356→- `--cgroup_mem_max` — cgroup-level memory enforcement (not just rlimit advisory limits)
   357→- seccomp-bpf syscall filtering — block `fork()`, `exec()`, network syscalls
   358→
   359→bubblewrap provides namespace isolation but relies on external tooling for resource limits. For sandboxing untrusted agent-generated code, resource limits are non-negotiable.
   360→
   361→### 6.2 Implementation
   362→
   363→**File:** `services/rune-agent/src/rune_agent/nodes.py`
   364→
   365→```python
   366→# New: sandbox configuration
   367→SANDBOX_BACKENDS = {
   368→    "subprocess": _exec_subprocess,   # current behavior, no isolation
   369→    "nsjail": _exec_nsjail,           # namespace + resource limits
   370→}
   371→
   372→async def execute_node(state: RuneState) -> dict[str, Any]:
   373→    backend = os.environ.get("RUNE_EXEC_BACKEND", "subprocess")
   374→    executor = SANDBOX_BACKENDS[backend]
   375→    return await executor(state)
   376→
   377→
   378→def _build_nsjail_cmd(script_path: str, tmpdir: str) -> list[str]:
   379→    timeout = int(os.environ.get("RUNE_EXEC_TIMEOUT", "30"))
   380→    mem_mb = int(os.environ.get("RUNE_EXEC_MEM_MB", "512"))
   381→
   382→    return [
   383→        "nsjail",
   384→        "--mode", "o",                      # execute once
   385→        "--time_limit", str(timeout),        # wall-time
   386→        "--rlimit_as", str(mem_mb),          # address space limit
   387→        "--rlimit_cpu", str(timeout),        # CPU time limit
   388→        "--rlimit_fsize", "10",              # max file size 10MB
   389→        "--rlimit_nofile", "32",             # max open files
   390→        "--disable_clone_newnet",            # OR: keep network blocked:
   391→        # "--clone_newnet",                  # no network access
   392→        "--chroot", "/",                     # minimal chroot
   393→        "--rw",                              # allow writes to tmpdir
   394→        "--bindmount", f"{tmpdir}:{tmpdir}", # mount workspace
   395→        "--", "python3", script_path,
   396→    ]
   397→
   398→
   399→async def _exec_nsjail(state: RuneState) -> dict[str, Any]:
   400→    timeout = int(os.environ.get("RUNE_EXEC_TIMEOUT", "30"))
   401→    script = state["generated_code"] + "\n\n" + state["test_suite"]
   402→
   403→    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:
   404→        script_path = os.path.join(tmpdir, "solution.py")
   405→        with open(script_path, "w") as f:
   406→            f.write(script)
   407→
   408→        cmd = _build_nsjail_cmd(script_path, tmpdir)
   409→
   410→        try:
   411→            proc = subprocess.run(
   412→                cmd,
   413→                capture_output=True,
   414→                text=True,
   415→                timeout=timeout + 5,  # grace period beyond nsjail's limit
   416→                cwd=tmpdir,
   417→            )
   418→            stdout = proc.stdout
   419→            stderr = proc.stderr
   420→            exit_code = proc.returncode
   421→            tests_passed = proc.returncode == 0
   422→        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   423→            stdout = ""
   424→            stderr = f"nsjail execution timed out after {timeout}s"
   425→            exit_code = 1
   426→            tests_passed = False
   427→
   428→    return {
   429→        "stdout": stdout,
   430→        "stderr": stderr,
   431→        "exit_code": exit_code,
   432→        "tests_passed": tests_passed,
   433→    }
   434→```
   435→
   436→### 6.3 Overhead Comparison
   437→
   438→| Backend | Startup | Memory overhead | Network isolation | Resource limits |
   439→|---|---|---|---|---|
   440→| subprocess (current) | ~1ms | None | No | No |
   441→| nsjail | ~5ms | ~2MB | Yes | Yes (rlimit + cgroup) |
   442→| Docker | ~500-1000ms | ~50MB | Yes | Yes |
   443→
   444→nsjail is **100-200× faster** than Docker at startup. Over 700 iterations per agent, that's ~3.5 seconds total overhead vs ~350-700 seconds for Docker.
   445→
   446→### 6.4 Installation
   447→
   448→nsjail is a single static binary. No daemon, no service.
   449→
   450→```dockerfile
   451→# In Dockerfile or system setup
   452→RUN apt-get install -y nsjail
   453→# Or build from source: https://github.com/google/nsjail
   454→```
   455→
   456→---
   457→
   458→## 7. Hardware-Adaptive Concurrency
   459→
   460→### 7.1 HardwareProbe
   461→
   462→```python
   463→# libs/shared/src/shared/hardware.py
   464→
   465→@dataclass
   466→class HardwareBudget:
   467→    max_agents: int           # concurrent LangGraph loops
   468→    max_concurrent_loras: int # vLLM adapter slots
   469→    training_slots: int       # ProcessPoolExecutor workers
   470→    vram_per_gpu_mb: int      # for config validation
   471→    single_gpu_mode: bool     # enables sleep/wake coordination
   472→
   473→@dataclass
   474→class HardwareProbe:
   475→    cpu_count: int
   476→    ram_total_mb: int
   477→    gpus: list[GPUInfo]  # name, vram_mb, compute_capability
   478→
   479→    @classmethod
   480→    def detect(cls) -> "HardwareProbe":
   481→        import psutil
   482→        cpu_count = psutil.cpu_count(logical=False) or 2
   483→        ram_total_mb = psutil.virtual_memory().total // (1024 * 1024)
   484→
   485→        gpus = []
   486→        try:
   487→            import pynvml
   488→            pynvml.nvmlInit()
   489→            for i in range(pynvml.nvmlDeviceGetCount()):
   490→                handle = pynvml.nvmlDeviceGetHandleByIndex(i)
   491→                info = pynvml.nvmlDeviceGetMemoryInfo(handle)
   492→                gpus.append(GPUInfo(
   493→                    name=pynvml.nvmlDeviceGetName(handle),
   494→                    vram_mb=info.total // (1024 * 1024),
   495→                ))
   496→            pynvml.nvmlShutdown()
   497→        except Exception:
   498→            pass  # CPU-only mode
   499→
   500→        return cls(cpu_count=cpu_count, ram_total_mb=ram_total_mb, gpus=gpus)
   501→
   502→    def compute_budget(self, base_model_vram_mb: int = 4500) -> HardwareBudget:
   503→        # Agent count: each agent needs ~1-2GB for Python + LangGraph state
   504→        # Reserve 2 cores for vLLM and training overhead
   505→        agent_by_cpu = max(1, self.cpu_count - 2)
   506→        agent_by_ram = max(1, (self.ram_total_mb - 4096) // 1500)  # 1.5GB per agent
   507→        max_agents = min(agent_by_cpu, agent_by_ram)
   508→
   509→        if not self.gpus:
   510→            return HardwareBudget(
   511→                max_agents=max_agents,
   512→                max_concurrent_loras=0,
   513→                training_slots=0,
   514→                vram_per_gpu_mb=0,
   515→                single_gpu_mode=True,
   516→            )
   517→
   518→        primary_vram = self.gpus[0].vram_mb
   519→        kv_cache_reserve = 1500  # MB, for reasonable context lengths
   520→        lora_size_mb = 15  # rank-64 adapter on 7B model
   521→
   522→        free_for_loras = primary_vram - base_model_vram_mb - kv_cache_reserve
   523→        max_loras = max(1, min(8, free_for_loras // lora_size_mb))  # cap at vLLM max
   524→
   525→        num_gpus = len(self.gpus)
   526→        single_gpu = num_gpus < 2
   527→
   528→        # Training slots: if single GPU, we time-share (1 slot);
   529→        # if multi-GPU, dedicate non-primary GPUs to training
   530→        training_slots = 1 if single_gpu else (num_gpus - 1)
   531→
   532→        return HardwareBudget(
   533→            max_agents=max_agents,
   534→            max_concurrent_loras=max_loras,
   535→            training_slots=training_slots,
   536→            vram_per_gpu_mb=primary_vram,
   537→            single_gpu_mode=single_gpu,
   538→        )
   539→```
   540→
   541→### 7.2 Example Budgets
   542→
   543→| Hardware | Agents | LoRA slots | Training | GPU mode |
   544→|---|---|---|---|---|
   545→| Laptop (8 core, 16GB, RTX 3060 12GB) | 3 | 6 | 1 (time-shared) | sleep/wake |
   546→| Workstation (16 core, 64GB, 1× RTX 4090 24GB) | 8 | 8 | 1 (time-shared) | sleep/wake |
   547→| Workstation (16 core, 64GB, 2× RTX 4090) | 8 | 8 | 1 (dedicated GPU 1) | parallel |
   548→| Server (32 core, 128GB, 4× A100 80GB) | 15 | 8 | 3 (GPUs 1-3) | parallel |
   549→| CPU-only (8 core, 32GB) | 4 | 0 | 0 | Ollama fallback |
   550→
   551→### 7.3 Dynamic Scaling
   552→
   553→The hardware probe runs once at startup. However, RAM pressure can change during a run (e.g., training workers loading models). The orchestrator monitors memory via a lightweight watchdog:
   554→
   555→```python
   556→async def memory_watchdog(budget: HardwareBudget, agents: list[Task]):
   557→    """Shed agents if system memory drops below 15% free."""
   558→    while True:
   559→        await asyncio.sleep(30)
   560→        mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
   561→        if mem.percent > 85:
   562→            # Gracefully stop the youngest agent
   563→            if agents:
   564→                agents[-1].cancel()
   565→                agents.pop()
   566→                logger.warning("Memory pressure: shed agent, %d remaining", len(agents))
   567→```
   568→
   569→---
   570→
   571→## 8. Registry as Message Board (refined from v1)
   572→
   573→### 8.1 Schema Extensions
   574→
   575→**File:** `libs/adapter-registry/src/adapter_registry/models.py`
   576→
   577→```python
   578→class AdapterRecord(SQLModel, table=True):
   579→    # ... existing fields (id, version, task_type, base_model_id, rank,
   580→    #     created_at, file_path, file_hash, file_size_bytes,
   581→    #     pass_rate, fitness_score, source, session_id, is_archived) ...
   582→
   583→    # Lineage tracking (replaces git DAG)
   584→    parent_ids: str | None = Field(default=None)     # JSON list of parent adapter IDs
   585→    generation: int = Field(default=0)                # evolutionary generation number
   586→
   587→    # Swarm coordination
   588→    training_task_hash: str | None = Field(default=None, index=True)  # dedup key
   589→    agent_id: str | None = Field(default=None)        # which swarm agent produced this
   590→```
   591→
   592→### 8.2 New Registry Methods
   593→
   594→**File:** `libs/adapter-registry/src/adapter_registry/registry.py`
   595→
   596→```python
   597→def query_best_for_task(self, task_type: str, top_k: int = 3) -> list[AdapterRecord]:
   598→    """Agents call this instead of reading a message board.
   599→    Returns top-k adapters by fitness_score for the given task type."""
   600→    with Session(self._engine) as session:
   601→        stmt = (
   602→            select(AdapterRecord)
   603→            .where(AdapterRecord.task_type == task_type)
   604→            .where(AdapterRecord.is_archived == False)
   605→            .order_by(AdapterRecord.fitness_score.desc())
   606→            .limit(top_k)
   607→        )
   608→        results = session.exec(stmt).all()
   609→        for r in results:
   610→            session.expunge(r)
   611→        return list(results)
   612→
   613→def is_task_solved(self, task_hash: str, threshold: float = 0.95) -> bool:
   614→    """Dedup: skip tasks another agent already solved well."""
   615→    with Session(self._engine) as session:
   616→        stmt = (
   617→            select(AdapterRecord)
   618→            .where(AdapterRecord.training_task_hash == task_hash)
   619→            .where(AdapterRecord.pass_rate >= threshold)
   620→        )
   621→        return session.exec(stmt).first() is not None
   622→
   623→def get_lineage(self, adapter_id: str) -> list[AdapterRecord]:
   624→    """Trace evolutionary ancestry (replaces git log)."""
   625→    lineage = []
   626→    current_id = adapter_id
   627→    while current_id:
   628→        record = self.retrieve_by_id(current_id)
   629→        if not record:
   630→            break
   631→        lineage.append(record)
   632→        parent_ids = json.loads(record.parent_ids) if record.parent_ids else []
   633→        current_id = parent_ids[0] if parent_ids else None
   634→    return lineage
   635→```
   636→
   637→### 8.3 Concurrency Note
   638→
   639→SQLite WAL is sufficient. Agents primarily read (`query_best_for_task`). Writes only happen when training completes (~5-10/hour). One change needed: explicit pool sizing.
   640→
   641→```python
   642→# registry.py constructor
   643→engine = create_engine(url, pool_size=20, max_overflow=10)
   644→```
   645→
   646→---
   647→
   648→## 9. Evolution Operator as Merge (refined from v1)
   649→
   650→### 9.1 Event-Driven + Periodic Hybrid
   651→
   652→The evolution worker runs as an asyncio task inside the orchestrator (Tier-1, fail-fast if it dies).
   653→
   654→```python
   655→async def evolution_worker(
   656→    registry: AdapterRegistry,
   657→    interval_seconds: float = 7200,  # 2 hours
   658→):
   659→    """Tier-1: event-driven eval on new adapters + periodic merge/prune sweep."""
   660→    last_sweep = time.monotonic()
   661→
   662→    while True:
   663→        await asyncio.sleep(60)  # check every minute
   664→
   665→        # Event-driven: evaluate any unevaluated adapters
   666→        unevaluated = registry.query_unevaluated()
   667→        for adapter in unevaluated:
   668→            await evaluate_adapter(adapter, registry)
   669→
   670→        # Periodic sweep: merge and prune
   671→        elapsed = time.monotonic() - last_sweep
   672→        if elapsed >= interval_seconds:
   673→            await evolution_sweep(registry)
   674→            last_sweep = time.monotonic()
   675→
   676→
   677→async def evolution_sweep(registry: AdapterRegistry):
   678→    """Merge top adapters per task_type, prune underperformers."""
   679→    for task_type in registry.get_task_types():
   680→        adapters = registry.query_by_task_type(task_type)
   681→
   682→        # Merge: if ≥5 adapters exist, TIES-merge top 3
   683→        if len(adapters) >= 5:
   684→            top_3 = sorted(adapters, key=lambda a: a.fitness_score)[-3:]
   685→            merged_id = ties_merge_adapters([a.id for a in top_3], task_type)
   686→            await evaluate_adapter(registry.retrieve_by_id(merged_id), registry)
   687→
   688→        # Prune: archive anything below threshold
   689→        for a in adapters:
   690→            if a.fitness_score is not None and a.fitness_score < 0.3:
   691→                registry.archive(a.id)
   692→```
   693→
   694→### 9.2 No Separate Service Needed
   695→
   696→In v1, evolution-svc was a separate FastAPI process. Since we're collapsing to a single process, evolution becomes a coroutine inside the orchestrator that directly calls `registry` methods and `model_training.merging` functions. No HTTP serialization.
   697→
   698→**Files to implement:**
   699→- `libs/model-training/src/model_training/merging.py` — TIES/DARE merge operations
   700→- `libs/evaluation/src/evaluation/ood_benchmark.py` — OOD generalization tasks
   701→- Evolution logic lives in `scripts/swarm.py` (or extracted to `libs/evolution/`)
   702→
   703→---
   704→
   705→## 10. File Modification Map
   706→
   707→### New Files
   708→
   709→| File | Purpose |

   711→| `scripts/swarm.py` | Fat orchestrator: agents, training, evolution, hardware probe |
   712→| `scripts/swarm_config.yaml` | Swarm configuration (overrides auto-detected budget) |
   713→| `libs/shared/src/shared/hardware.py` | HardwareProbe + HardwareBudget |
   714→| `libs/model-training/src/model_training/merging.py` | TIES/DARE adapter merge |

   738→Note: `training-svc` and `evolution-svc` remain as standalone services for non-swarm use (single interactive sessions). The swarm bypasses them.
   739→
   740→---
   741→
   742→## 11. Phased Implementation Roadmap
   743→
   744→### Phase 25: HardwareProbe + nsjail Sandbox (1 day)
   745→
   746→**Goal:** Hardware-adaptive concurrency detection and secure code execution.
   747→
   748→1. Implement `libs/shared/src/shared/hardware.py` — HardwareProbe, HardwareBudget
   749→2. Add nsjail backend to `execute_node()` in `rune-agent/nodes.py`
   750→3. Add `RUNE_EXEC_BACKEND` env var with `subprocess` (default) and `nsjail` options
   751→4. Write nsjail protobuf config for Rune's security profile
   752→5. Tests: HardwareProbe on CI (mocked GPU), nsjail sandbox on Linux
   753→
   754→**Success criteria:** `HardwareProbe.detect().compute_budget()` returns correct values on test hardware. nsjail sandbox rejects memory bombs and infinite loops.
   755→
   756→### Phase 26: Swarm Orchestrator + Two-Tier Exception Hierarchy (2 days)
   757→
   758→**Goal:** N agents running concurrently with fault isolation and crash recovery.
   759→
   760→1. Build `scripts/swarm.py` — orchestrator with TaskGroup + agent supervisors
   761→2. Implement SwarmCheckpointDB (SQLite) for crash recovery
   762→3. Implement agent supervisor loop with backoff
   763→4. Implement task queue with registry-based dedup (`is_task_solved()`)
   764→5. Wire `query_best_for_task()` for weight-space discovery
   765→6. Tests: 2-agent swarm on toy tasks; inject failure in one agent, verify other continues
   766→
   767→**Success criteria:** `python scripts/swarm.py --agents=2 --hours=0.1` runs, one agent crash doesn't kill the other, checkpoint recovery works on restart.
   768→
   769→### Phase 27: Training Workers + GPU Time-Sharing (2 days)
   770→
   771→**Goal:** QLoRA training runs out-of-process; vLLM sleep/wake cycle works on single GPU.
   772→
   773→1. Implement training_pool_manager with ProcessPoolExecutor
   774→2. Implement vLLM sleep/wake coordination for single-GPU mode
   775→3. Implement multi-GPU path (skip sleep/wake, dedicate GPUs)
   776→4. Add `_train_in_subprocess()` top-level function
   777→5. Wire training completion → registry.store()
   778→6. Tests: single-GPU sleep/wake cycle; verify agents resume after training
   779→
   780→**Success criteria:** Training runs in separate process. On single GPU: vLLM sleeps → training runs → vLLM wakes → agents resume. No OOM.
   781→
   782→### Phase 28: Evolution Worker + Adapter Merging (2 days)
   783→
   784→**Goal:** Automated fitness evaluation, TIES-merge, and pruning.
   785→
   786→1. Implement evolution_worker coroutine (event-driven eval + periodic sweep)
   787→2. Implement `libs/model-training/merging.py` — TIES-merge, DARE-merge
   788→3. Implement `libs/evaluation/ood_benchmark.py` — OOD task set
   789→4. Extend AdapterRecord with lineage/swarm columns
   790→5. Implement `query_unevaluated()`, `archive()`, `get_lineage()`
   791→6. Tests: register adapter → evaluate → merge top-3 → verify merged fitness
   792→
   793→**Success criteria:** New adapter auto-evaluated. Sweep merges top-3 when cohort reaches 5. Low-fitness adapters archived.
   794→
   795→### Phase 29: Integration Test + Overnight Run (1 day)
   796→
   797→**Goal:** Full end-to-end validation on real hardware.
   798→
   799→1. Run full swarm overnight on HumanEval task pool
   800→2. Measure: adapters produced, fitness distribution, merge quality, GPU utilization
   801→3. Compare: swarm-produced adapters vs single-agent baseline
   802→4. Stress test: verify memory watchdog sheds agents under pressure
   803→5. Document results in `docs/article/results.md`
   804→
   805→**Success criteria:** Swarm produces measurably better adapters than single-agent loop. No OOM. Crash recovery works.
   806→
   807→---
   808→
   809→## 12. Risk Matrix (Updated)
   810→
   811→| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |
   812→|---|---|---|---|
   813→| ProcessPoolExecutor pickle errors | Medium | Medium | All training args are primitives (str/int/float); test pickle boundary explicitly |
   814→| vLLM sleep/wake segfault on exit | Low | Low | Known issue (#16993); catch in finally block, restart vLLM if needed |
   815→| nsjail not available on macOS | High | Low | Fallback to `subprocess` backend; nsjail only on Linux (production path) |
   816→| Single-process crash loses all state | Medium | High | SQLite checkpoint table; swarm.py is restartable |
   817→| Memory watchdog too aggressive | Low | Medium | 15% free threshold tunable; only sheds youngest agent |
   818→| TIES-merge produces degenerate adapter | Medium | Medium | Evaluate merged adapter before promoting; keep parents |
   819→| Training worker subprocess OOM | Medium | Medium | `--rlimit_as` on training process; graceful failure → retry |
   820→
   821→---
   822→
   823→## 13. What's Eliminated vs v1
   824→
   825→| v1 Component | v2 Status | Reason |
   826→|---|---|---|
   827→| Redis | **Eliminated** | No inter-service queue needed in single-process model |
   828→| ARQ task queue | **Eliminated** | Replaced by asyncio.Queue + ProcessPoolExecutor |
   829→| training-svc FastAPI (for swarm) | **Bypassed** | Direct function calls; HTTP path kept for interactive use |
   830→| evolution-svc FastAPI (for swarm) | **Bypassed** | Coroutine inside orchestrator |
   831→| Docker sandboxes | **Eliminated** | nsjail: 100-200× faster, same isolation |
   832→| events-py Redis transport | **Eliminated** | Events are direct function calls within single process |
   833→| Hardcoded concurrency | **Eliminated** | HardwareProbe auto-detects at startup |
   834→| asyncio.TaskGroup for agents | **Replaced** | Two-tier hierarchy: supervisors never propagate |
   835→
   836→**Net new dependencies:** `nsjail` (system binary), `psutil` (pip), `pynvml` (pip, optional).
   837→
   838→---
   839→
   840→## 14. Non-Goals (Deferred)
   841→
   842→- **Distributed multi-machine swarm** — deferred to v7+
   843→- **Replacing SQLite with Postgres** — unnecessary at local scale
   844→- **LangGraph modifications** — graph.py stays untouched
   845→- **Human-in-the-loop during swarm** — overnight runs are fully autonomous
   846→- **vLLM colocate mode** — requires vLLM in-process (incompatible with our separate-process model); revisit if TRL integration deepens
   847→
   848→---
   849→
   850→_v2 compresses the architecture from 5 HTTP-connected services to a single process with one external boundary (vLLM). It replaces Docker with nsjail (200× faster), BackgroundTasks with ProcessPoolExecutor (GIL-free), and hardcoded concurrency with hardware probing. The two-tier exception hierarchy ensures one bad agent never kills the swarm._
   851→
   852→---
   853→
   854→**References:**
   855→
   856→- [vLLM Sleep Mode Documentation](https://docs.vllm.ai/en/latest/features/sleep_mode/)
   857→- [vLLM Sleep Mode Blog (benchmarks)](https://vllm.ai/blog/sleep-mode)
   858→- [Co-located vLLM in TRL (HuggingFace)](https://huggingface.co/blog/vllm-colocate)
   859→- [GRPO + vLLM Training Cookbook](https://huggingface.co/learn/cookbook/grpo_vllm_online_training)
   860→- [nsjail: Google's process isolation tool](https://github.com/google/nsjail)
   861→- [Anthropic Sandbox Runtime (bubblewrap)](https://github.com/anthropic-experimental/sandbox-runtime)
   862→- [Karpathy autoresearch](https://github.com/karpathy/autoresearch)

> AGENT

You're right — the whole point of v6 was collapsing to `swarm.py` as the fat orchestrator. No FastAPI, no microservices. The agent loop uses the LangGraph graph directly, inference goes through vLLM/Ollama via the provider abstraction, and code runs in the sandbox.

The demo should use `swarm.py` — not a standalone FastAPI app as the *task*. The model should solve pure algorithmic/coding problems that get executed in the sandbox, generate trajectories, and feed the training loop.

Let me rewrite the demo properly — using the swarm with a task pool of coding problems, run through the actual `agent_supervisor` → LangGraph loop → sandbox pipeline.

     1→"""Fat orchestrator for parallel swarm execution.
     2→
     3→Collapses Rune's microservice architecture into a single-process
     4→orchestrator for local hardware. Coordinates agent supervisors,
     5→training pool, evolution worker, and memory watchdog via asyncio.
     6→
     7→Usage:
     8→    uv run scripts/swarm.py [--config scripts/swarm_config.yaml] [--agents N] [--hours H] [--dry-run]
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import argparse
    14→import asyncio
    15→import json
    16→import logging
    17→import sys
    18→import uuid
    19→from pathlib import Path
    20→from typing import Any
    21→
    22→import yaml
    23→
    24→# Add scripts dir to path for sibling imports
    25→sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))
    26→
    27→from shared.checkpoint_db import SwarmCheckpointDB
    28→from shared.hardware import HardwareProbe
    29→from shared.rune_models import SwarmConfig
    30→from shared.sandbox import get_sandbox_backend
    31→from swarm_evolution import evolution_worker
    32→from swarm_workers import TrainingRequest, memory_watchdog, training_pool_manager
    33→
    34→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    35→
    36→
    37→def load_task_pool(task_source: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    38→    """Load tasks from a JSON file or return empty list.
    39→
    40→    Args:
    41→        task_source: Path to a JSON file containing task definitions.
    42→
    43→    Returns:
    44→        List of task dicts with at minimum 'task_id' and 'description'.
    45→    """
    46→    path = Path(task_source)
    47→    if path.exists():
    48→        with path.open() as f:
    49→            return json.load(f)
    50→    logger.warning("Task source %s not found, using empty task pool", task_source)
    51→    return []
    52→
    53→
    54→async def agent_supervisor(
    55→    agent_id: str,
    56→    tasks: list[dict[str, Any]],
    57→    checkpoint_db: SwarmCheckpointDB,
    58→    training_queue: asyncio.Queue[TrainingRequest],
    59→    dry_run: bool = False,
    60→    max_failures: int = 5,
    61→    shutdown_event: asyncio.Event | None = None,
    62→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    63→    """Tier-2 supervisor for a single swarm agent.
    64→
    65→    Processes tasks from the pool, checking checkpoints for dedup,
    66→    running code via sandbox, and queuing training requests.
    67→
    68→    Args:
    69→        agent_id: Unique identifier for this agent.
    70→        tasks: List of task dicts to process.
    71→        checkpoint_db: Checkpoint DB for dedup and recovery.
    72→        training_queue: Queue for submitting training requests.
    73→        dry_run: If True, skip actual execution.
    74→        max_failures: Consecutive failures before retiring.
    75→        shutdown_event: Event to signal graceful shutdown.
    76→
    77→    Returns:
    78→        Summary dict with completed/failed/skipped counts.
    79→    """
    80→    completed = 0
    81→    failed = 0
    82→    skipped = 0
    83→    consecutive_failures = 0
    84→    sandbox = get_sandbox_backend()
    85→
    86→    for task in tasks:
    87→        if shutdown_event and shutdown_event.is_set():
    88→            break
    89→
    90→        task_hash = task.get("task_id", str(uuid.uuid4()))
    91→
    92→        if checkpoint_db.is_completed(task_hash):
    93→            skipped += 1
    94→            continue
    95→
    96→        checkpoint_db.mark_running(task_hash, agent_id)
    97→
    98→        if dry_run:
    99→            checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task_hash, "dry-run")
   100→            completed += 1
   101→            consecutive_failures = 0
   102→            continue
   103→
   104→        try:
   105→            code = task.get("prompt", "") + task.get("canonical_solution", "")
   106→            test = task.get("test", "")
   107→            script = code + "\n" + test
   108→            result = sandbox.run(script, timeout=30)
   109→
   110→            if result.exit_code == 0 and not result.timed_out:
   111→                checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task_hash, "success")
   112→                completed += 1
   113→                consecutive_failures = 0
   114→                await training_queue.put(
   115→                    TrainingRequest(session_id=task_hash, task_type="swarm")
   116→                )
   117→            else:
   118→                checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task_hash, agent_id)
   119→                failed += 1
   120→                consecutive_failures += 1
   121→        except Exception:
   122→            logger.exception("Agent %s failed on task %s", agent_id, task_hash)
   123→            checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task_hash, agent_id)
   124→            failed += 1
   125→            consecutive_failures += 1
   126→
   127→        if consecutive_failures >= max_failures:
   128→            logger.warning(
   129→                "Agent %s retiring after %d consecutive failures",
   130→                agent_id,
   131→                max_failures,
   132→            )
   133→            break
   134→
   135→        # Backoff on failure
   136→        if consecutive_failures > 0:
   137→            await asyncio.sleep(min(2**consecutive_failures, 30))
   138→
   139→    return {
   140→        "agent_id": agent_id,
   141→        "completed": completed,
   142→        "failed": failed,
   143→        "skipped": skipped,
   144→    }
   145→
   146→
   147→async def run_swarm(config: SwarmConfig, dry_run: bool = False) -> None:
   148→    """Tier-1 orchestrator — runs the full swarm with asyncio.TaskGroup.
   149→
   150→    Args:
   151→        config: Swarm configuration.
   152→        dry_run: If True, skip actual execution and training.
   153→    """
   154→    from adapter_registry import AdapterRegistry
   155→    from sqlmodel import create_engine
   156→
   157→    logger.info("Starting swarm with config: %s", config.model_dump())
   158→
   159→    # Detect hardware
   160→    probe = HardwareProbe.detect()
   161→    budget = probe.compute_budget()
   162→    logger.info("Hardware: %s, Budget: %s", probe, budget)
   163→
   164→    # Initialize registry and checkpoint DB
   165→    registry_engine = create_engine(config.db_url)
   166→    registry = AdapterRegistry(engine=registry_engine)
   167→    checkpoint_engine = create_engine("sqlite:///swarm_checkpoints.db")
   168→    checkpoint_db = SwarmCheckpointDB(checkpoint_engine)
   169→
   170→    # Load tasks
   171→    tasks = load_task_pool(config.task_source)
   172→    if not tasks:
   173→        logger.warning("No tasks to process")
   174→        return
   175→
   176→    # Shared state
   177→    training_queue: asyncio.Queue[TrainingRequest] = asyncio.Queue()
   178→    shutdown_event = asyncio.Event()
   179→    num_agents = min(config.population_size, budget.max_agents)
   180→
   181→    # Partition tasks across agents
   182→    partitions: list[list[dict[str, Any]]] = [[] for _ in range(num_agents)]
   183→    for i, task in enumerate(tasks):
   184→        partitions[i % num_agents].append(task)
   185→
   186→    async with asyncio.TaskGroup() as tg:
   187→        # Agent supervisors
   188→        agent_tasks = []
   189→        for i in range(num_agents):
   190→            t = tg.create_task(
   191→                agent_supervisor(
   192→                    agent_id=f"agent-{i}",
   193→                    tasks=partitions[i],
   194→                    checkpoint_db=checkpoint_db,
   195→                    training_queue=training_queue,
   196→                    dry_run=dry_run,
   197→                    shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
   198→                )
   199→            )
   200→            agent_tasks.append(t)
   201→
   202→        # Training pool (background)
   203→        tg.create_task(
   204→            training_pool_manager(
   205→                training_queue=training_queue,
   206→                budget=budget,
   207→                db_url=config.db_url,
   208→                shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
   209→            )
   210→        )
   211→
   212→        # Evolution worker (background)
   213→        tg.create_task(
   214→            evolution_worker(
   215→                registry=registry,
   216→                interval_seconds=config.evolution_interval,
   217→                shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
   218→            )
   219→        )
   220→
   221→        # Memory watchdog (background)
   222→        tg.create_task(
   223→            memory_watchdog(
   224→                budget=budget,
   225→                agents=agent_tasks,
   226→                shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
   227→            )
   228→        )
   229→
   230→        # Wait for all agents to finish, then shutdown background tasks
   231→        done = await asyncio.gather(*agent_tasks, return_exceptions=True)
   232→        shutdown_event.set()
   233→
   234→        for result in done:
   235→            if isinstance(result, Exception):
   236→                logger.error("Agent failed: %s", result)
   237→            else:
   238→                logger.info("Agent result: %s", result)
   239→
   240→
   241→def main() -> None:
   242→    """CLI entry point for the swarm orchestrator."""
   243→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Rune Swarm Orchestrator")
   244→    parser.add_argument(
   245→        "--config",
   246→        default="scripts/swarm_config.yaml",
   247→        help="Path to swarm config YAML",
   248→    )
   249→    parser.add_argument("--agents", type=int, help="Override population_size")
   250→    parser.add_argument("--hours", type=float, help="Max runtime in hours")
   251→    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Skip execution")
   252→    args = parser.parse_args()
   253→
   254→    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
   255→
   256→    config_path = Path(args.config)
   257→    if config_path.exists():
   258→        raw = yaml.safe_load(config_path.read_text())
   259→        config = SwarmConfig(**raw)
   260→    else:
   261→        config = SwarmConfig()
   262→
   263→    if args.agents:
   264→        config.population_size = args.agents
   265→
   266→    asyncio.run(run_swarm(config, dry_run=args.dry_run))
   267→
   268→
   269→if __name__ == "__main__":
   270→    main()
   271→
```

     1→"""State definition for the Rune coding agent recursive loop."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Optional
     6→
     7→from typing_extensions import TypedDict
     8→
     9→
    10→class RuneState(TypedDict):
    11→    """State for the Rune coding agent recursive loop.
    12→
    13→    Attributes:
    14→        task_description: Natural language description of the coding task.
    15→        task_type: Category of task (e.g. 'function', 'class', 'refactor').
    16→        test_suite: Test code that the generated solution must pass.
    17→        adapter_ids: LoRA adapter IDs to load for parametric memory.
    18→        session_id: Unique identifier for trajectory persistence (UUID4).
    19→        attempt_count: Current attempt number (0-indexed, incremented by reflect).
    20→        max_attempts: Maximum number of generation attempts allowed.
    21→        generated_code: Code produced by the generate node.
    22→        stdout: Standard output from executing generated code.
    23→        stderr: Standard error from executing generated code.
    24→        exit_code: Process exit code from execution (0 = success).
    25→        tests_passed: Whether the test suite passed on this attempt.
    26→        trajectory: List of per-attempt records for parametric memory training.
    27→        outcome: Terminal result -- 'success', 'exhausted', or None if still running.
    28→    """
    29→
    30→    # Task intake

     1→"""Fat orchestrator for parallel swarm execution.
     2→
     3→Collapses Rune's microservice architecture into a single-process
     4→orchestrator for local hardware. Coordinates agent supervisors,
     5→training pool, evolution worker, and memory watchdog via asyncio.
     6→
     7→Usage:
     8→    uv run scripts/swarm.py [--config scripts/swarm_config.yaml] [--agents N] [--hours H] [--dry-run]
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import argparse
    14→import asyncio
    15→import json
    16→import logging
    17→import sys
    18→import uuid
    19→from pathlib import Path
    20→from typing import Any
    21→
    22→import yaml
    23→
    24→# Add workspace src dirs and scripts dir to path
    25→_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
    26→for _src in [
    27→    "services/rune-agent/src",
    28→    "libs/inference/src",
    29→    "libs/shared/src",
    30→    "libs/model-training/src",
    31→    "libs/adapter-registry/src",
    32→    "libs/evaluation/src",
    33→]:
    34→    sys.path.insert(0, str(_root / _src))
    35→sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))
    36→
    37→from shared.checkpoint_db import SwarmCheckpointDB
    38→from shared.hardware import HardwareProbe
    39→from shared.rune_models import SwarmConfig
    40→from shared.sandbox import get_sandbox_backend
    41→from swarm_evolution import evolution_worker
    42→from swarm_workers import TrainingRequest, memory_watchdog, training_pool_manager
    43→
    44→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    45→
    46→
    47→def load_task_pool(task_source: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    48→    """Load tasks from a JSON file or return empty list.
    49→
    50→    Args:
    51→        task_source: Path to a JSON file containing task definitions.
    52→
    53→    Returns:
    54→        List of task dicts with at minimum 'task_id' and 'description'.
    55→    """
    56→    path = Path(task_source)
    57→    if path.exists():
    58→        with path.open() as f:
    59→            return json.load(f)
    60→    logger.warning("Task source %s not found, using empty task pool", task_source)
    61→    return []
    62→
    63→
    64→async def agent_supervisor(
    65→    agent_id: str,
    66→    tasks: list[dict[str, Any]],
    67→    checkpoint_db: SwarmCheckpointDB,
    68→    training_queue: asyncio.Queue[TrainingRequest],
    69→    dry_run: bool = False,
    70→    max_failures: int = 5,
    71→    shutdown_event: asyncio.Event | None = None,
    72→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    73→    """Tier-2 supervisor for a single swarm agent.
    74→
    75→    Processes tasks from the pool, checking checkpoints for dedup,
    76→    running code via sandbox, and queuing training requests.
    77→
    78→    Args:
    79→        agent_id: Unique identifier for this agent.
    80→        tasks: List of task dicts to process.
    81→        checkpoint_db: Checkpoint DB for dedup and recovery.
    82→        training_queue: Queue for submitting training requests.
    83→        dry_run: If True, skip actual execution.
    84→        max_failures: Consecutive failures before retiring.
    85→        shutdown_event: Event to signal graceful shutdown.
    86→
    87→    Returns:
    88→        Summary dict with completed/failed/skipped counts.
    89→    """
    90→    completed = 0
    91→    failed = 0
    92→    skipped = 0
    93→    consecutive_failures = 0
    94→    sandbox = get_sandbox_backend()
    95→
    96→    for task in tasks:
    97→        if shutdown_event and shutdown_event.is_set():
    98→            break
    99→
   100→        task_hash = task.get("task_id", str(uuid.uuid4()))
   101→
   102→        if checkpoint_db.is_completed(task_hash):
   103→            skipped += 1
   104→            continue
   105→
   106→        checkpoint_db.mark_running(task_hash, agent_id)
   107→
   108→        if dry_run:
   109→            checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task_hash, "dry-run")
   110→            completed += 1
   111→            consecutive_failures = 0
   112→            continue
   113→
   114→        try:
   115→            code = task.get("prompt", "") + task.get("canonical_solution", "")
   116→            test = task.get("test", "")
   117→            script = code + "\n" + test
   118→            result = sandbox.run(script, timeout=30)
   119→
   120→            if result.exit_code == 0 and not result.timed_out:
   121→                checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task_hash, "success")
   122→                completed += 1
   123→                consecutive_failures = 0
   124→                await training_queue.put(
   125→                    TrainingRequest(session_id=task_hash, task_type="swarm")
   126→                )
   127→            else:
   128→                checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task_hash, agent_id)
   129→                failed += 1
   130→                consecutive_failures += 1
   131→        except Exception:
   132→            logger.exception("Agent %s failed on task %s", agent_id, task_hash)
   133→            checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task_hash, agent_id)
   134→            failed += 1
   135→            consecutive_failures += 1
   136→
   137→        if consecutive_failures >= max_failures:
   138→            logger.warning(
   139→                "Agent %s retiring after %d consecutive failures",
   140→                agent_id,
   141→                max_failures,
   142→            )
   143→            break
   144→
   145→        # Backoff on failure
   146→        if consecutive_failures > 0:
   147→            await asyncio.sleep(min(2**consecutive_failures, 30))
   148→
   149→    return {
   150→        "agent_id": agent_id,
   151→        "completed": completed,
   152→        "failed": failed,
   153→        "skipped": skipped,
   154→    }
   155→
   156→
   157→async def run_swarm(config: SwarmConfig, dry_run: bool = False) -> None:
   158→    """Tier-1 orchestrator — runs the full swarm with asyncio.TaskGroup.
   159→
   160→    Args:
   161→        config: Swarm configuration.
   162→        dry_run: If True, skip actual execution and training.
   163→    """
   164→    from adapter_registry import AdapterRegistry
   165→    from sqlmodel import create_engine
   166→
   167→    logger.info("Starting swarm with config: %s", config.model_dump())
   168→
   169→    # Detect hardware
   170→    probe = HardwareProbe.detect()
   171→    budget = probe.compute_budget()
   172→    logger.info("Hardware: %s, Budget: %s", probe, budget)
   173→
   174→    # Initialize registry and checkpoint DB
   175→    registry_engine = create_engine(config.db_url)
   176→    registry = AdapterRegistry(engine=registry_engine)
   177→    checkpoint_engine = create_engine("sqlite:///swarm_checkpoints.db")
   178→    checkpoint_db = SwarmCheckpointDB(checkpoint_engine)
   179→
   180→    # Load tasks
   181→    tasks = load_task_pool(config.task_source)
   182→    if not tasks:
   183→        logger.warning("No tasks to process")
   184→        return
   185→
   186→    # Shared state
   187→    training_queue: asyncio.Queue[TrainingRequest] = asyncio.Queue()
   188→    shutdown_event = asyncio.Event()
   189→    num_agents = min(config.population_size, budget.max_agents)
   190→
   191→    # Partition tasks across agents
   192→    partitions: list[list[dict[str, Any]]] = [[] for _ in range(num_agents)]
   193→    for i, task in enumerate(tasks):
   194→        partitions[i % num_agents].append(task)
   195→
   196→    async with asyncio.TaskGroup() as tg:
   197→        # Agent supervisors
   198→        agent_tasks = []
   199→        for i in range(num_agents):
   200→            t = tg.create_task(
   201→                agent_supervisor(
   202→                    agent_id=f"agent-{i}",
   203→                    tasks=partitions[i],
   204→                    checkpoint_db=checkpoint_db,
   205→                    training_queue=training_queue,
   206→                    dry_run=dry_run,
   207→                    shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
   208→                )
   209→            )
   210→            agent_tasks.append(t)
   211→
   212→        # Training pool (background)
   213→        tg.create_task(
   214→            training_pool_manager(
   215→                training_queue=training_queue,
   216→                budget=budget,
   217→                db_url=config.db_url,
   218→                shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
   219→            )
   220→        )
   221→
   222→        # Evolution worker (background)
   223→        tg.create_task(
   224→            evolution_worker(
   225→                registry=registry,
   226→                interval_seconds=config.evolution_interval,
   227→                shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
   228→            )
   229→        )
   230→
   231→        # Memory watchdog (background)
   232→        tg.create_task(
   233→            memory_watchdog(
   234→                budget=budget,
   235→                agents=agent_tasks,
   236→                shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
   237→            )
   238→        )
   239→
   240→        # Wait for all agents to finish, then shutdown background tasks
   241→        done = await asyncio.gather(*agent_tasks, return_exceptions=True)
   242→        shutdown_event.set()
   243→
   244→        for result in done:
   245→            if isinstance(result, Exception):
   246→                logger.error("Agent failed: %s", result)
   247→            else:
   248→                logger.info("Agent result: %s", result)
   249→
   250→
   251→def main() -> None:
   252→    """CLI entry point for the swarm orchestrator."""
   253→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Rune Swarm Orchestrator")
   254→    parser.add_argument(
   255→        "--config",
   256→        default="scripts/swarm_config.yaml",
   257→        help="Path to swarm config YAML",
   258→    )
   259→    parser.add_argument("--agents", type=int, help="Override population_size")
   260→    parser.add_argument("--hours", type=float, help="Max runtime in hours")
   261→    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Skip execution")
   262→    args = parser.parse_args()
   263→
   264→    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
   265→
   266→    config_path = Path(args.config)
   267→    if config_path.exists():
   268→        raw = yaml.safe_load(config_path.read_text())
   269→        config = SwarmConfig(**raw)
   270→    else:
   271→        config = SwarmConfig()
   272→
   273→    if args.agents:
   274→        config.population_size = args.agents
   275→
   276→    asyncio.run(run_swarm(config, dry_run=args.dry_run))
   277→
   278→
   279→if __name__ == "__main__":
   280→    main()
   281→
```

     1→"""Training pool management and memory watchdog for swarm execution.
     2→
     3→Provides async workers for managing concurrent training jobs with GPU
     4→time-sharing (sleep/wake for single-GPU mode) and memory pressure monitoring.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import asyncio
    10→import logging
    11→from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass
    13→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
    14→
    15→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    16→    from shared.hardware import HardwareBudget
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass
    22→class TrainingRequest:
    23→    """A request to train a LoRA adapter.
    24→
    25→    Attributes:
    26→        session_id: Unique session identifier.
    27→        task_type: Task category for training.
    28→    """
    29→
    30→    session_id: str
    31→    task_type: str
    32→
    33→
    34→async def _drain_queue(
    35→    queue: asyncio.Queue[TrainingRequest],
    36→    max_batch: int,
    37→    timeout: float = 1.0,
    38→) -> list[TrainingRequest]:
    39→    """Drain up to max_batch items from an async queue.
    40→
    41→    Args:
    42→        queue: Async queue to drain.
    43→        max_batch: Maximum items to collect.
    44→        timeout: Seconds to wait for the first item.
    45→
    46→    Returns:
    47→        List of collected TrainingRequest items.
    48→    """
    49→    batch: list[TrainingRequest] = []
    50→    try:
    51→        first = await asyncio.wait_for(queue.get(), timeout=timeout)
    52→        batch.append(first)
    53→    except (asyncio.TimeoutError, TimeoutError):
    54→        return batch
    55→
    56→    while len(batch) < max_batch:
    57→        try:
    58→            item = queue.get_nowait()
    59→            batch.append(item)
    60→        except asyncio.QueueEmpty:
    61→            break
    62→    return batch
    63→
    64→
    65→def _train_in_subprocess(
    66→    session_id: str,
    67→    task_type: str,
    68→    adapter_dir: str,
    69→    db_url: str,
    70→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    71→    """Top-level picklable function for training in a subprocess.
    72→
    73→    Imports GPU modules inside function body (INFRA-05 pattern).
    74→
    75→    Args:
    76→        session_id: Unique session identifier.
    77→        task_type: Task category.
    78→        adapter_dir: Directory to save adapter weights.
    79→        db_url: Database URL for the adapter registry.
    80→
    81→    Returns:
    82→        Dict with adapter_id, session_id, task_type, adapter_dir.
    83→    """
    84→    import uuid
    85→
    86→    adapter_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    87→
    88→    try:
    89→        from model_training.trainer import train_qlora
    90→
    91→        train_qlora(
    92→            session_id=session_id,
    93→            task_type=task_type,
    94→            output_dir=adapter_dir,
    95→        )
    96→    except ImportError:
    97→        logger.warning(
    98→            "model_training.trainer not available; skipping actual training "
    99→            "for session %s",
   100→            session_id,
   101→        )
   102→
   103→    return {
   104→        "adapter_id": adapter_id,
   105→        "session_id": session_id,
   106→        "task_type": task_type,
   107→        "adapter_dir": adapter_dir,
   108→    }
   109→
   110→
   111→async def _sleep_vllm(base_url: str) -> None:
   112→    """Tell vLLM to release GPU memory for training.
   113→
   114→    Args:
   115→        base_url: vLLM server base URL.
   116→    """
   117→    try:
   118→        import httpx
   119→
   120→        async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
   121→            await client.post(f"{base_url}/sleep", timeout=30)
   122→    except Exception:
   123→        logger.warning("Failed to sleep vLLM at %s", base_url)
   124→
   125→
   126→async def _wake_vllm(base_url: str) -> None:
   127→    """Tell vLLM to reclaim GPU memory after training.
   128→
   129→    Args:
   130→        base_url: vLLM server base URL.
   131→    """
   132→    try:
   133→        import httpx
   134→
   135→        async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
   136→            await client.post(f"{base_url}/wake_up", timeout=30)
   137→    except Exception:
   138→        logger.warning("Failed to wake vLLM at %s", base_url)
   139→
   140→
   141→async def training_pool_manager(
   142→    training_queue: asyncio.Queue[TrainingRequest],
   143→    budget: "HardwareBudget",
   144→    db_url: str = "sqlite:///rune_swarm.db",
   145→    adapter_base_dir: str = "/tmp/rune_adapters",
   146→    vllm_base_url: str = "http://localhost:8100/v1",
   147→    shutdown_event: asyncio.Event | None = None,
   148→) -> None:
   149→    """Async manager that processes training requests from a queue.
   150→
   151→    Uses ProcessPoolExecutor for CPU-bound training. In single-GPU mode,
   152→    coordinates with vLLM via sleep/wake to time-share the GPU.
   153→
   154→    Args:
   155→        training_queue: Queue of TrainingRequest items.
   156→        budget: Hardware budget for concurrency decisions.
   157→        db_url: Database URL for the adapter registry.
   158→        adapter_base_dir: Base directory for saving adapter weights.
   159→        vllm_base_url: vLLM server URL for sleep/wake coordination.
   160→        shutdown_event: Event to signal graceful shutdown.
   161→    """
   162→    loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   163→    max_workers = max(1, budget.training_slots)
   164→
   165→    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers) as pool:
   166→        while True:
   167→            if shutdown_event and shutdown_event.is_set():
   168→                break
   169→
   170→            batch = await _drain_queue(
   171→                training_queue, max_batch=max_workers, timeout=2.0
   172→            )
   173→            if not batch:
   174→                continue
   175→
   176→            # Single-GPU: sleep vLLM before training
   177→            if budget.single_gpu_mode and budget.training_slots > 0:
   178→                await _sleep_vllm(vllm_base_url)
   179→
   180→            futures = []
   181→            for req in batch:
   182→                adapter_dir = f"{adapter_base_dir}/{req.session_id}"
   183→                future = loop.run_in_executor(
   184→                    pool,
   185→                    _train_in_subprocess,
   186→                    req.session_id,
   187→                    req.task_type,
   188→                    adapter_dir,
   189→                    db_url,
   190→                )
   191→                futures.append(future)
   192→
   193→            results = await asyncio.gather(*futures, return_exceptions=True)
   194→
   195→            # Single-GPU: wake vLLM after training
   196→            if budget.single_gpu_mode and budget.training_slots > 0:
   197→                await _wake_vllm(vllm_base_url)
   198→
   199→            for result in results:
   200→                if isinstance(result, Exception):
   201→                    logger.error("Training failed: %s", result)
   202→                else:
   203→                    logger.info("Training completed: %s", result)
   204→
   205→
   206→async def memory_watchdog(
   207→    budget: "HardwareBudget",
   208→    agents: list[asyncio.Task[Any]],
   209→    check_interval: float = 30.0,
   210→    threshold: float = 0.85,
   211→    shutdown_event: asyncio.Event | None = None,
   212→) -> None:
   213→    """Monitor memory pressure and cancel youngest agent if too high.
   214→
   215→    Args:
   216→        budget: Hardware budget (unused but kept for future VRAM checks).
   217→        agents: Mutable list of running agent tasks.
   218→        check_interval: Seconds between memory checks.
   219→        threshold: Memory usage fraction triggering cancellation.
   220→        shutdown_event: Event to signal graceful shutdown.
   221→    """
   222→    import psutil
   223→
   224→    while True:
   225→        if shutdown_event and shutdown_event.is_set():
   226→            break
   227→
   228→        mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
   229→        usage = mem.percent / 100.0
   230→
   231→        if usage > threshold and agents:
   232→            youngest = agents[-1]
   233→            logger.warning(
   234→                "Memory pressure %.1f%% > %.1f%% threshold, cancelling youngest agent",
   235→                usage * 100,
   236→                threshold * 100,
   237→            )
   238→            youngest.cancel()
   239→            agents.remove(youngest)
   240→
   241→        await asyncio.sleep(check_interval)
   242→
```

     1→"""Evolution worker and sweep logic for swarm adapter evolution.
     2→
     3→Provides async workers that periodically evaluate adapters, merge
     4→top performers via TIES-Merging, and prune low-fitness adapters.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import asyncio
    10→import logging
    11→import uuid
    12→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
    13→
    14→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    15→    from adapter_registry import AdapterRegistry
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→# Thresholds
    20→MERGE_MIN_ADAPTERS = 5
    21→MERGE_TOP_K = 3
    22→PRUNE_FITNESS_THRESHOLD = 0.3
    23→
    24→
    25→def evaluate_adapter(
    26→    adapter_id: str,
    27→    pass_rate: float,
    28→    diversity_score: float,
    29→    registry: "AdapterRegistry",
    30→) -> float:
    31→    """Evaluate an adapter and update its fitness in the registry.
    32→
    33→    Args:
    34→        adapter_id: ID of the adapter to evaluate.
    35→        pass_rate: Measured pass rate.
    36→        diversity_score: Measured diversity score.
    37→        registry: Adapter registry for persisting results.
    38→
    39→    Returns:
    40→        Computed fitness score.
    41→    """
    42→    from evaluation.metrics import evaluate_fitness
    43→
    44→    fitness = evaluate_fitness(adapter_id, pass_rate, diversity_score)
    45→    registry.update_fitness(adapter_id, pass_rate=pass_rate, fitness_score=fitness)
    46→    return fitness
    47→
    48→
    49→def evolution_sweep(registry: "AdapterRegistry") -> dict[str, Any]:
    50→    """Run one evolution sweep across all task types.
    51→
    52→    For each task type with >= MERGE_MIN_ADAPTERS, TIES-merges the top-3
    53→    adapters. Archives any adapter with fitness < PRUNE_FITNESS_THRESHOLD.
    54→
    55→    Args:
    56→        registry: Adapter registry to query and modify.
    57→
    58→    Returns:
    59→        Summary dict with merged and pruned counts per task type.
    60→    """
    61→    summary: dict[str, Any] = {"task_types": {}}
    62→
    63→    for task_type in registry.get_task_types():
    64→        all_adapters = registry.query_by_task_type(task_type)
    65→        active = [a for a in all_adapters if not a.is_archived]
    66→
    67→        merged_count = 0
    68→        pruned_count = 0
    69→
    70→        # Merge top adapters if we have enough
    71→        if len(active) >= MERGE_MIN_ADAPTERS:
    72→            top = registry.query_best_for_task(task_type, top_k=MERGE_TOP_K)
    73→            if len(top) >= 2:
    74→                parent_ids = [a.id for a in top]
    75→                try:
    76→                    _ties_merge_adapters(parent_ids, task_type, registry)
    77→                    merged_count = 1
    78→                except Exception:
    79→                    logger.exception("TIES merge failed for task_type=%s", task_type)
    80→
    81→        # Prune low-fitness adapters
    82→        for adapter in active:
    83→            if (
    84→                adapter.fitness_score is not None
    85→                and adapter.fitness_score < PRUNE_FITNESS_THRESHOLD
    86→            ):
    87→                registry.archive(adapter.id)
    88→                pruned_count += 1
    89→
    90→        summary["task_types"][task_type] = {
    91→            "merged": merged_count,
    92→            "pruned": pruned_count,
    93→            "active": len(active) - pruned_count + merged_count,
    94→        }
    95→
    96→    return summary
    97→
    98→
    99→def _ties_merge_adapters(
   100→    adapter_ids: list[str],
   101→    task_type: str,
   102→    registry: "AdapterRegistry",
   103→) -> str:
   104→    """Load adapter state dicts, TIES-merge them, and register the result.
   105→
   106→    All GPU imports are deferred (INFRA-05 pattern).
   107→
   108→    Args:
   109→        adapter_ids: IDs of adapters to merge.
   110→        task_type: Task type for the merged adapter.
   111→        registry: Registry for loading adapter paths and storing result.
   112→
   113→    Returns:
   114→        ID of the newly registered merged adapter.
   115→    """
   116→    import json
   117→
   118→    from model_training.merging import load_adapter_state_dict, ties_merge
   119→
   120→    adapters = [registry.retrieve_by_id(aid) for aid in adapter_ids]
   121→    state_dicts = [load_adapter_state_dict(a.file_path) for a in adapters]
   122→    merged_sd = ties_merge(state_dicts)
   123→
   124→    # Save merged adapter
   125→    merged_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
   126→    output_dir = f"/tmp/rune_merged/{merged_id}"
   127→
   128→    import os
   129→
   130→    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
   131→
   132→    from safetensors.torch import save_file
   133→
   134→    save_file(merged_sd, f"{output_dir}/adapter_model.safetensors")
   135→
   136→    # Register merged adapter
   137→    from adapter_registry.models import AdapterRecord
   138→
   139→    max_gen = max(a.generation for a in adapters)
   140→    record = AdapterRecord(
   141→        id=merged_id,
   142→        version=1,
   143→        task_type=task_type,
   144→        base_model_id=adapters[0].base_model_id,
   145→        rank=adapters[0].rank,
   146→        created_at="",
   147→        file_path=f"{output_dir}/adapter_model.safetensors",
   148→        file_hash="",
   149→        file_size_bytes=0,
   150→        source="evolution",
   151→        session_id="merge",
   152→        parent_ids=json.dumps(adapter_ids),
   153→        generation=max_gen + 1,
   154→    )
   155→    registry.store(record)
   156→    return merged_id
   157→
   158→
   159→async def evolution_worker(
   160→    registry: "AdapterRegistry",
   161→    interval_seconds: int = 7200,
   162→    shutdown_event: asyncio.Event | None = None,
   163→) -> None:
   164→    """Async worker that runs periodic evolution sweeps.
   165→
   166→    Args:
   167→        registry: Adapter registry to operate on.
   168→        interval_seconds: Seconds between sweep runs.
   169→        shutdown_event: Event to signal graceful shutdown.
   170→    """
   171→    while True:
   172→        if shutdown_event and shutdown_event.is_set():
   173→            break
   174→
   175→        try:
   176→            summary = evolution_sweep(registry)
   177→            logger.info("Evolution sweep completed: %s", summary)
   178→        except Exception:
   179→            logger.exception("Evolution sweep failed")
   180→
   181→        await asyncio.sleep(interval_seconds)
   182→
```
